L1 DIFF
  EN: ## 12. Implementation Plan and Schedule
  ZH: ## 12. 實施計劃與進度表
L2 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L3 DIFF
  EN: ### 12.1 Delivery Approach
  ZH: ### 12.1 交付方針
L4 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L5 DIFF
  EN: The project is delivered through an **incremental, phase-gated build**. The estate described in chapters 3 to 11 is not constructed in one pass and then tested at the end; it is constructed in seven phases, each of which ends at a **verification gate** that must be passed, with evidence committed to `C:\FYP\06-verification\`, before the next phase is entered. The approach follows five principles that are fixed by the design baseline.
  ZH: 本項目以**增量式、階段閘門（phase-gated）建構**的方式交付。第 3 至 11 章所述的整體環境並非一次過建成然後在最後才測試；而是分為七個階段建構，每個階段在進入下一階段之前，必須通過一道**驗證閘門（verification gate）**，並將證據提交至 `C:\FYP\06-verification\`。此方針依循設計基準所固定的五項原則。
L6 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L7 DIFF
  EN: **1. Freeze the identity layer first.** Nothing else in the design is stable until addressing, naming and the domain exist. The canonical baseline (`00-design-baseline.md`) is frozen as `v00-baseline-v1.0` at the end of week 1, and its §16 interface contract — hostnames, IP addresses, VLAN identifiers, AS numbers, service-account names, GPO names, VRID values and the banned-string list — is designated non-negotiable for individual writers. This is the project's principal anti-inconsistency mechanism: four people building four parts of one design will diverge unless a single frozen set of values is copied, not re-invented. The baseline records the mechanism explicitly: any change to §16 requires a decision-log entry, three-of-four group agreement, supervisor awareness and a re-issued revision, because *"silent deviation from §16 is a defect."*
  ZH: **1. 先凍結身分層。** 在定址、命名及網域存在之前，設計中沒有任何其他部分是穩定的。權威基準（`00-design-baseline.md`）於第 1 週結束時凍結為 `v00-baseline-v1.0`，其 §16 介面契約——主機名稱、IP 位址、VLAN 識別碼、AS 號碼、服務帳戶名稱、GPO 名稱、VRID 值以及禁用字串清單——指定為個別撰寫者不可協商的部分。這是本項目對抗不一致的主要機制：四個人建構同一份設計的四個部分，除非複製同一套已凍結的數值而非各自重新發明，否則必然出現分歧。基準明確記錄此機制：§16 的任何變更均須有決策日誌記錄、四人中三人的小組同意、導師知情，以及重新簽發修訂版本，因為*「對 §16 的靜默偏離即屬缺陷」*。
L8 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L9 DIFF
  EN: **2. Build one layer, prove it, then build on it.** The service dependency chain fixed in the baseline runs *hypervisor host → management zone (`vpnz-hvhost01`, 10.20.20.10) → `vpnz-dc01` (DNS, AD DS, NTP, CA) → server-zone services → the nftables egress pair → the ASA → the simulated provider → the simulated Internet*. Each phase adds exactly one link to that chain. This matters practically: a later fault cannot be caused by a layer that was already proven, so every phase boundary narrows the search space for the next fault. It also means the weekly gate evidence doubles as troubleshooting evidence for Domain 6 of the standard (*Troubleshooting*, 25 %).
  ZH: **2. 先建一層、證明該層，再在其上建構。** 基準所固定的服務相依鏈為*主機虛擬化平台 → 管理區域（`vpnz-hvhost01`，10.20.20.10）→ `vpnz-dc01`（DNS、AD DS、NTP、CA）→ 伺服器區域服務 → nftables 出口閘道對 → ASA → 模擬供應商 → 模擬互聯網*。每個階段只為該鏈增加恰好一個環節。這在實務上很重要：後續的故障不可能是由已證明過的層所引起，因此每個階段邊界都縮小了下一項故障的搜尋空間。這也意味著每週的閘門證據同時可作為標準中 Domain 6（*Troubleshooting（疑難排解）*，25 %）的排錯證據。
L10 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L11 DIFF
  EN: **3. Simulated and genuinely configured work run in parallel but are tested differently.** All Cisco routing, switching and firewall behaviour is simulated in Cisco Packet Tracer, while the VM estate — Active Directory, DNS, DHCP, FSRM, iSCSI, RRAS, Nginx, Keepalived, nftables, OpenVPN, WireGuard, Easy-RSA, the monitoring stack, Ansible and the agent runtime — is genuinely configured and running. The two tracks need different test levels (section 13.1) and must never be reported in the same voice: a convergence figure taken from Packet Tracer is a simulation artefact, and the baseline requires it to be labelled as such.
  ZH: **3. 模擬工作與真實配置工作並行推進，但以不同方式測試。** 所有 Cisco 路由、交換及防火牆行為均在 Cisco Packet Tracer 中模擬，而 VM 環境——Active Directory、DNS、DHCP、FSRM、iSCSI、RRAS、Nginx、Keepalived、nftables、OpenVPN、WireGuard、Easy-RSA、監控堆疊、Ansible 及代理執行環境——則是真實配置並實際運行。這兩條軌道需要不同的測試層級（第 13.1 節），且絕不可用同一種語氣報告：取自 Packet Tracer 的收斂數據屬於模擬產物，基準要求必須如此標註。
L12 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L13 DIFF
  EN: **4. Two-track automation honesty.** Packet Tracer exposes no API and no SSH server reachable from real tooling, and no Ansible collection can drive it. The automation line is therefore delivered in two declared tracks: the playbooks and scripts are **written, executed and idempotence-tested** against the Linux and Windows estate, while the *intent* for network devices is held as text configuration in Git under `03-build/packet-tracer/configs/*.txt` and applied through the simulator's own CLI, with a written gap analysis of exactly which step a real device would require (`netmiko`/`napalm` over SSH). The proposal states plainly that **no playbook in this project ever logs in to a Packet Tracer device**.
  ZH: **4. 雙軌自動化的誠實表述。** Packet Tracer 並無對外 API，亦無可供真實工具連接的 SSH 伺服器，且沒有任何 Ansible collection 可以驅動它。因此自動化線以兩條明示軌道交付：playbook 與腳本**針對 Linux 及 Windows 環境進行撰寫、執行及冪等性測試**，而網路裝置的*意圖*則以文字配置形式保存於 Git 的 `03-build/packet-tracer/configs/*.txt`，並透過模擬器自身的 CLI 套用，同時附上書面差距分析，說明真實裝置將需要哪一個具體步驟（透過 SSH 使用 `netmiko`／`napalm`）。提案明確聲明：**本項目中沒有任何 playbook 會登入 Packet Tracer 裝置**。

  EN:

L15 DIFF
  EN: **5. The schedule is protected by a fixed priority order, not by optimism.** The baseline fixes four cut bands — **must** (L1 core roles; L3 OSPF, RSTP, ASA; L2 Nginx, Keepalived, nftables; L4 three playbooks), **should** (EIGRP/BGP, iSCSI, monitoring, WordPress), **could** (voice, two-tier PKI, Restic, MCP tool bridge) and **won't** (everything in the baseline's "NOT COVERED" rows). Section 12.9 turns that order into an explicit cut sequence.
  ZH: **5. 進度表靠固定的優先次序保護，而非靠樂觀心態。** 基準固定了四個削減帶——**必須**（L1 核心角色；L3 OSPF、RSTP、ASA；L2 Nginx、Keepalived、nftables；L4 三個 playbook）、**應該**（EIGRP／BGP、iSCSI、監控、WordPress）、**可以**（語音、兩層 PKI、Restic、MCP 工具橋接）以及**不會做**（基準「NOT COVERED」列中的所有項目）。第 12.9 節將該次序轉化為明確的削減順序。

L159 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L160 DIFF
  EN: **Hypervisor and template availability (W1–W2) → AD DS, DNS and DHCP operational (W3) → internal PKI issuing certificates (W3–W4) → Nginx TLS termination and the DMZ web farm (W5–W7) → nftables egress pair with NAT, required before any published service can be reached from the simulated Internet (W6) → ASA ACL, NAT and site-to-site IPsec (W7–W8) → end-to-end published service path through the proxy (W7) → full verification execution (W11) → documentation close-out and acceptance (W12).**
  ZH: **虛擬化平台與範本可用（W1–W2）→ AD DS、DNS 及 DHCP 投入運作（W3）→ 內部 PKI 簽發憑證（W3–W4）→ Nginx TLS 終結與 DMZ Web 伺服器陣列（W5–W7）→ 具 NAT 的 nftables 出口對，此為任何已發佈服務能從模擬互聯網觸及之前必需（W6）→ ASA ACL、NAT 及站對站 IPsec（W7–W8）→ 經代理的端到端已發佈服務路徑（W7）→ 完整驗證執行（W11）→ 文件結案與驗收（W12）。**
L161 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L162 DIFF
  EN: The reason this, and not the Cisco routing work, is critical is that the *published-service acceptance criterion* depends on four independent things being simultaneously correct: name resolution, TLS material from the PKI, the reverse-proxy tier with a working VIP, and an egress path that permits the flow. Routing scale (EIGRP, BGP) is on a secondary path because the portal can be demonstrated with OSPF alone; it strengthens Domain 3 evidence but does not gate the primary acceptance criterion.
  ZH: 這條路徑（而非 Cisco 路由工作）之所以關鍵，原因在於*已發佈服務驗收標準*取決於四件互相獨立的事情同時正確：名稱解析、來自 PKI 的 TLS 材料、具可用 VIP 的反向代理層，以及允許該流量的出口路徑。路由規模化（EIGRP、BGP）位於次要路徑，因為入口網站僅憑 OSPF 即可演示；它強化了 Domain 3 的證據，但不構成主要驗收標準的閘門。

L187 DIFF
  EN: **Float management.** Only four work packages have genuinely useful float (WP-05 at five days, WP-12 and WP-13 at six days each, WP-17 at six days). Total float across the secondary path is therefore small, and the group treats any consumption beyond half of it as a trigger for the re-planning procedure in section 12.9 rather than as absorbable slack. Negative float is avoided by protecting WP-01, WP-03, WP-04 and WP-07 with the pairing arrangement in section 12.5 rather than by adding buffer that does not exist.
  ZH: **浮時管理。** 只有四個工作包擁有真正有用的浮時（WP-05 為五天，WP-12 及 WP-13 各六天，WP-17 為六天）。因此次要路徑上的總浮時很小，小組將任何超過其中一半的消耗視為觸發第 12.9 節重新規劃程序的條件，而非可吸收的鬆餘。負浮時透過第 12.5 節的配對安排保護 WP-01、WP-03、WP-04 及 WP-07 來避免，而不是靠加入並不存在的緩衝。

L271 DIFF
  EN: **How the fifth line is shared, and why.** The AI line is the only line that has no single natural owner, because it is the only line that *consumes the other four rather than building anything of its own*. The agent runtime runs on one VM (`vpnz-ai01`) and its entire behaviour is reading: alerts from the monitoring stack, interface counters from network devices, failed-login events from the identity plane and the DMZ, and proposed diffs against configuration held in Git. Ownership is therefore shared along a clean interface:
  ZH: **第五條線如何共用，以及原因。** AI 線是唯一沒有單一自然擁有人的線，因為它是唯一*消耗另外四條線、而非自行建構任何東西*的線。代理執行環境運行於一部 VM（`vpnz-ai01`），其全部行為就是讀取：來自監控堆疊的告警、來自網路裝置的介面計數器、來自身分平面及 DMZ 的登入失敗事件，以及針對 Git 中所保存配置的建議差異。因此擁有權沿著一條清楚的介面共用：

  EN: - **Member D** owns the **agent side** — the runtime, the four read-only tool manifests, the allow-list and rate limit, the audit log in `/opt/openclaw/logs/agent.jsonl`, the prompt and evaluation records, and the honesty statement about which fallback was taken.

  EN:

L277 DIFF
  EN: The interface between the two owners is a **frozen tool contract**: four tools, no fifth tool, each with a declared input schema and a declared read-only behaviour. Because the contract is frozen before either side writes code, the two owners can work in parallel without integration surprises — and any change to it is a §16-class change requiring a baseline revision.
  ZH: 兩位擁有人之間的介面是一份**凍結的工具契約**：四個工具，沒有第五個，每個工具都有已聲明的輸入 schema 及已聲明的唯讀行為。由於契約在任一方撰寫程式碼之前已凍結，兩位擁有人可以並行工作而不致出現整合意外——而對它的任何變更均屬 §16 級別變更，須重新簽發基準。

  EN:

L279 DIFF
  EN: Two consequences are worth stating in the proposal rather than discovering at assessment. First, **the AI line is the weakest line and is deliberately scoped as a proof of concept with declared limits**, because the standard on which this project is aligned contains no AI content at all: `Agentic AI`, `OpenClaw`, `MCP`, `artificial intelligence` and `machine learning` each occur **zero times** in the WSOS 2026 page, in both the extracted text and the raw HTML. The AI work is project innovation beyond the standard, and it is presented that way. Second, the line is protected by five pre-designed fallbacks (F1 to F5 in the baseline), and the fallback must be **chosen by the end of week 6**, not improvised in week 11.
  ZH: 有兩項後果值得在提案中說明，而不是在評核時才發現。第一，**AI 線是最弱的一條線，且刻意界定為附有聲明限制的概念驗證**，因為本項目所對齊的標準完全不包含 AI 內容：`Agentic AI`、`OpenClaw`、`MCP`、`artificial intelligence` 及 `machine learning` 在 WSOS 2026 頁面中各出現**零次**，在擷取文字及原始 HTML 中皆然。AI 工作屬於超越該標準的項目創新，並以此方式呈現。第二，該線由五個預先設計的備援方案保護（基準中的 F1 至 F5），且備援方案必須**在第 6 週結束前選定**，而非在第 11 週臨時拼湊。

  EN: #### 12.5.3 How the split keeps each member's contribution individually assessable
  ZH: #### 12.5.3 此分工如何使每名成員的貢獻可個別評核
L282 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L283 DIFF
  EN: Individual contribution normally has to be evidenced, and four people working on one integrated system makes that genuinely difficult. This project answers it with five overlapping mechanisms, so that no single mechanism has to carry the whole argument:
  ZH: 個人貢獻通常必須有證據支持，而四個人共同處理一套整合系統使這確實困難。本項目以五項互相重疊的機制應對，使沒有任何單一機制必須承擔整個論證：
L284 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L285 DIFF
  EN: 1. **Named personal administrative accounts.** Every member has exactly one `adm-{initials}` account in `OU=Admins`, two of the four are additionally in `GG-Tier0-Admins`, and the identity design requires that no shared administrative account is used in normal operation. Administrative events therefore carry a username, and the security log is evidence of who did what.
  ZH: 1. **具名的個人管理帳戶。** 每名成員在 `OU=Admins` 中恰好有一個 `adm-{initials}` 帳戶，四人中有兩人另屬 `GG-Tier0-Admins`，且身分設計要求正常運作時不得使用任何共用管理帳戶。因此管理事件均帶有使用者名稱，而安全日誌即為誰做了什麼的證據。
L286 DIFF
  EN: 2. **One accountable owner and one reviewer per deliverable, recorded in the matrix.** Section 12.5.1 assigns a single **A** per row. Where a row lists two members (**R**/**R**), the accountable member is still singular.
  ZH: 2. **每項交付物有一名當責擁有人及一名審查者，並記錄於矩陣中。** 第 12.5.1 節為每一列指派單一的 **A**。即使某列列出兩名成員（**R**／**R**），當責成員仍然只有一人。
L287 DIFF
  EN: 3. **Authorship of report sections is fixed and disjoint.** Members A, B and C author their own technology chapters; Member D authors the automation, AI, verification and close-out chapters. The proposal's own section map shows this, and the final report repeats it.
  ZH: 3. **報告章節的撰寫權固定且互不重疊。** Member A、B 及 C 各自撰寫自己的技術章節；Member D 撰寫自動化、AI、驗證及結案章節。本提案自身的章節對照表顯示這一點，最終報告亦重述。
L288 DIFF
  EN: 4. **Evidence ownership.** Every screenshot and transcript in `07-evidence\` and `06-verification\` has an owner recorded in the master evidence index, and the verification record carries the initials of the person who performed the test. A test that nobody signed is not evidence.
  ZH: 4. **證據擁有權。** `07-evidence\` 及 `06-verification\` 中每張截圖及每份逐字稿，均在主證據索引中記錄擁有人，而驗證記錄載有執行該測試者的姓名縮寫。沒有人簽署的測試不是證據。

L304 DIFF
  EN: Cross-cover is made real, not nominal, by four rules:
  ZH: 交叉替補透過四項規則成為真實而非名義上的安排：
L305 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L306 DIFF
  EN: 1. **The shadow must have run every artefact at least once.** A playbook, script or configuration that only its owner has ever executed is not cross-covered, because nobody else knows how to restore it. Exercising the paired artefact once is a documented task, not an optional courtesy.
  ZH: 1. **影子成員必須至少各執行過每一件產物一次。** 只有其擁有人執行過的 playbook、腳本或配置，不算已交叉替補，因為沒有其他人知道如何還原它。將配對產物實際操演一次是一項有記錄的任務，而非可選的客套。
L307 DIFF
  EN: 2. **The peer-review control doubles as cross-training.** Every playbook and configuration change is reviewed by a second person before it is merged, and the review is recorded in the meeting minutes. This is a deliberate response to the scenario's due-diligence requirement for change applied through *reviewed, repeatable configuration* rather than by hand.
  ZH: 2. **同儕審查這項控制同時兼具交叉訓練作用。** 每一項 playbook 及配置變更在合併前均由第二人審查，而該審查記錄於會議紀錄中。這是對情境中所要求之盡職審查（due diligence）的刻意回應——變更須透過*經審查、可重複的配置*套用，而非手動操作。
L308 DIFF
  EN: 3. **No member holds the only copy of anything.** All artefacts are in Git on `vpnz-mon01` **and** mirrored to a second medium, so that a failed laptop loses work but not the project. No member is the sole holder of any artefact — this is an explicit baseline mitigation for the machine-failure risk.
  ZH: 3. **沒有任何成員持有任何事物的唯一副本。** 所有產物均存放於 `vpnz-mon01` 上的 Git **並**鏡像至第二種媒體，因此筆記型電腦故障會損失工作成果，但不會損失整個項目。沒有任何成員是任何產物的唯一持有人——這是基準針對機器故障風險的明確緩解措施。
L309 DIFF
  EN: 4. **The vault password and the break-glass password are held by two different people.** The Ansible Vault password is held by the group with a sealed copy deposited with the supervisor; the `da-vertexpeak` break-glass password is sealed in a physical envelope held by the supervisor and is not used during normal operation, with any use logged in the report.
  ZH: 4. **保管庫密碼與緊急破窗密碼由兩個不同的人持有。** Ansible Vault 密碼由小組持有，並將密封副本交存導師；`da-vertexpeak` 緊急破窗密碼密封於實體信封中由導師保管，正常運作期間不得使用，任何使用均記錄於報告中。

  EN: **What is recorded, and in what form.** Every meeting produces a dated entry containing: date and time; attendees and apologies; the agenda items; each decision in the form *decision — rationale — who is accountable — by when*; every action with an owner and a due date; and any change to the risk register or the issues log arising from it. Records live **inside the same repository** as the technical artefacts (`C:\FYP\01-proposal\`, `C:\FYP\02-design\`, `C:\FYP\06-verification\`) so that a reviewer can move from a weekly gate record to the evidence it cites to the configuration that produced it without leaving the repository.
  ZH: **記錄什麼，以及以何種形式記錄。** 每次會議產生一則有日期的紀錄，內容包括：日期及時間；出席者及缺席致意；議程項目；每項決定以*決定 — 理由 — 誰當責 — 何時完成*的形式記錄；每項行動均有擁有人及到期日；以及由此產生的任何風險登記冊或問題日誌變更。涉及介面契約的決定另於 `02-design\decision-log.md` 的決策日誌中記錄，並列明其所隱含的基準修訂版本。紀錄與技術產物存放於**同一個儲存庫**內（`C:\FYP\01-proposal\`、`C:\FYP\02-design\`、`C:\FYP\06-verification\`），使審查者可以從每週閘門記錄，走到其所引用的證據，再走到產生該證據的配置，而毋須離開儲存庫。

L375 DIFF
  EN: **Worked example (illustrative, using a real open item).** *CR-04 — WireGuard site-to-site peer representation.* Reason: a Cisco IOS device cannot terminate WireGuard, and the WireGuard brief technology is a "must" item. Description: adopt resolution option (a) from the baseline — represent the Wan Chai encrypting gateway as an additional Ubuntu virtual machine `vpnz-wcwg01` with the inner address 10.10.200.5 on the WAN transport network — rather than option (b), remote-workers-only WireGuard with IPsec on the ASA for the branch. Category: inventory, design. Artefacts affected: the VM inventory table, the WireGuard test cases, the naming section, the topology diagram, the L2 chapter. Impact: no effect on the critical path; adds one VM and roughly one half-day of build. Risk introduced: none (the host RAM budget is reviewed and the VM is shut down outside tunnel tests). Decision: **approved**, closing open item O-04 at the end of week 6. Rationale recorded: option (a) keeps WireGuard in the design as a genuinely configured technology, which is what the brief asks for, and keeps the tunnel comparison in chapter 5 honest. Re-verification: the WireGuard handshake test case is re-run and the raw baseline row is corrected so that the report and the design cannot contradict one another.
  ZH: **實例演練（示意，採用真實的待辦事項）。** *CR-04 — WireGuard 站對站對等方表示法。* 原因：Cisco IOS 裝置無法終結 WireGuard，而 WireGuard 是簡介中的「必須」項目。描述：採用基準中的解決方案選項 (a)——在 WAN 傳輸網路上以額外一部 Ubuntu 虛擬機 `vpnz-wcwg01`，並配置內部位址 10.10.200.5，代表灣仔的加密閘道——而非選項 (b)，即僅供遠端工作者使用的 WireGuard，並在分支的 ASA 上使用 IPsec。類別：清單、設計。受影響產物：VM 清單表、WireGuard 測試案例、命名章節、拓撲圖、L2 章節。影響：對關鍵路徑無影響；增加一部 VM 及約半日建構工時。引入的風險：無（已檢視主機 RAM 預算，且該 VM 在隧道測試以外時間關機）。決定：**批准**，於第 6 週結束時結案待辦事項 O-04。所記錄的理由：選項 (a) 使 WireGuard 作為真正配置的技術留在設計中，而這正是簡介所要求的，並使第 5 章的隧道比較保持誠實。重新驗證：重新執行 WireGuard 握手測試案例，並更正原始基準列，使報告與設計不可能互相矛盾。

L422 DIFF
  EN: The contingency plan is a **fixed cut order**, decided in advance. Cutting under pressure without a pre-agreed order produces inconsistent decisions, and the baseline's risk R-02 identifies schedule overrun from attempting full depth as a High/High risk. The order below is derived directly from the baseline's four cut bands.
  ZH: 應變計劃是**預先決定的固定削減順序**。在壓力下沒有預先協定的順序而作出削減，會導致決定不一致，而基準的風險 R-02 將「因試圖做到完整深度而導致的進度超支」列為 High／High 風險。以下順序直接衍生自基準的四個削減帶。

  EN: | A version pin cannot be met | Record the deviation, state its impact, and fall back to the named alternative (for example Windows Server 2022 in place of 2025, VirtualBox in place of Workstation Pro, or the distribution-packaged version of a component) | Owner of the affected line |
  ZH: | 無法達成某項版本鎖定 | 記錄偏差、聲明其影響，並退回至指名的替代方案（例如以 Windows Server 2022 代替 2025、以 VirtualBox 代替 Workstation Pro，或採用發行版套件版本的元件） | 受影響線的擁有人 |

  EN: | A Packet Tracer discrepancy consumes more than half a day | Stop, record the discrepancy as an experiment result, and move on — a documented discrepancy is legitimate academic content, whereas a lost week is not | Member C |
  ZH: | Packet Tracer 的某項差異耗用超過半日 | 停止，將該差異記錄為實驗結果，然後繼續——有記錄的差異是正當的學術內容，而白白損失一週則不是 | Member C |

L514 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L515 DIFF
  EN: **Test data.** All data is fictional and is generated by the group. There is no real customer data, no real personal data, and no real public address anywhere in the lab — public addresses come only from the documentation ranges, and published names end in `.example`. Test data consists of: a synthetic user population (roughly 160 fictional staff objects spread across the OU tree so that group-scope and delegation tests are meaningful); four FSRM shares with named quota templates and a blocked-file-type file screen; three iSCSI LUNs with generated content for the mount and reconnect tests; a generated ASP.NET sample application and a WordPress content set authored by the group; a deliberately long-lived certificate and a deliberately revoked one for the revocation and expiry tests; and, for the security tests, an intentionally isolated lab segment used as the source of the deliberate-misuse traffic.
  ZH: **測試資料。** 所有資料均屬虛構並由小組產生。實驗環境中沒有任何真實客戶資料、沒有任何真實個人資料，也沒有任何真實公眾位址——公眾位址僅取自文件專用範圍，而對外發佈的名稱以 `.example` 結尾。測試資料包括：一個合成使用者群（約 160 個虛構員工物件，散佈於 OU 樹中，使群組範圍及委派測試具意義）；四個 FSRM 共享，附具名配額範本及封鎖檔案類型的檔案屏蔽；三個 iSCSI LUN，附產生內容以供掛載及重新連接測試；一個產生的 ASP.NET 範例應用程式及一組由小組撰寫的 WordPress 內容；一張刻意長效的憑證及一張刻意撤銷的憑證，用於撤銷及到期測試；以及在安全測試中，一個刻意隔離的實驗室網段，用作刻意誤用流量的來源。

L517 DIFF
  EN: **Environment reset between major test cycles.** Snapshots are the reset mechanism, and their limitations are stated as prominently as their use:
  ZH: **主要測試週期之間的環境重設。** 快照是重設機制，而其限制與其用途同樣顯著地聲明：
L518 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L519 DIFF
  EN: 1. Take a named hypervisor snapshot of every VM in the scope of the cycle before the cycle begins, named `{yyyymmdd}-{cycle}-{host}`.
  ZH: 1. 在週期開始前，對週期範圍內每部 VM 建立具名虛擬化平台快照，命名為 `{yyyymmdd}-{cycle}-{host}`。
L520 DIFF
  EN: 2. Run the cycle. Record every test result against that snapshot baseline in the executed test record.
  ZH: 2. 執行該週期。在已執行測試記錄中，將每項測試結果對照該快照基準記錄。
L521 DIFF
  EN: 3. At the end of the cycle, **revert to the pristine snapshot** rather than repairing the estate, so the next cycle starts from a known state. Repairs are what make later tests irreproducible.
  ZH: 3. 週期結束時，**還原至初始快照**，而非修補環境，使下一個週期從已知狀態開始。修補正是使後續測試無法重現的原因。
L522 DIFF
  EN: 4. Where a configuration is managed by Ansible or a script, prefer re-running the automation over reverting the snapshot, because re-running also re-proves idempotence. Use the snapshot only where the change was manual.
  ZH: 4. 若某項配置由 Ansible 或腳本管理，優先重跑自動化而非還原快照，因為重跑同時再次證明冪等性。僅在變更屬手動時才使用快照。
L523 DIFF
  EN: 5. Export the current configuration of every network device and every service to the archive before reverting, so the previous cycle's evidence remains reconstructable.
  ZH: 5. 在還原之前，將每部網路裝置及每項服務的當前配置匯出至歸檔，使前一週期的證據仍可重建。
L524 DIFF
  EN: 6. Remove the snapshot once the cycle's evidence is complete and indexed. **Snapshots are never treated as a backup**: they share the same failure domain as the estate, they are not validated by a restore, and the design's backup path is the configuration and evidence backup to `D:\iSCSI\LUN02` and the off-host restic job to `vpnz-dmzjump01`.
  ZH: 6. 週期證據完成並索引後移除快照。**快照絕不視為備份**：它們與環境共用同一故障域，且未經還原驗證，而設計的備份路徑是至 `D:\iSCSI\LUN02` 的配置及證據備份，以及至 `vpnz-dmzjump01` 的離主機 restic 工作。
L525 DIFF
  EN: 7. For Packet Tracer, the reset mechanism is different and simpler: each test cycle starts from a named `.pkt` file committed to Git, so the topology and every device configuration are restored by opening a specific version.
  ZH: 7. 就 Packet Tracer 而言，重設機制不同且更簡單：每個測試週期均從已提交至 Git 的具名 `.pkt` 檔案開始，因此拓撲及每部裝置的配置均透過開啟特定版本而還原。

L527 DIFF
  EN: **Limitation to record with every result from this environment.** There is no physical hardware. No test in this project measures real forwarding hardware, real ASIC behaviour, real cabling, real power, real clock behaviour or real Internet connectivity. Packet Tracer results are simulator behaviour; VM results are hypervisor-mediated behaviour; and both are reported as such.
  ZH: **必須隨本環境每一項結果一併記錄的限制。** 本項目沒有實體硬體。本項目中沒有任何測試量測真實的轉發硬體、真實的 ASIC 行為、真實的佈線、真實的電力、真實的時鐘行為或真實的互聯網連線。Packet Tracer 的結果是模擬器行為；VM 的結果是經虛擬化平台中介的行為；兩者均如此報告。

  ZH: | TC-SEC-27 | 管理介面未暴露於較低信任區域 | 自管理區域以外，掃描管理位址及管理埠：`nmap -Pn -p 22,161,443,3389,5985,5986,6516 10.20.20.10-32`；自使用者區域、DMZ、實驗室網段及模擬互聯網重複 | 自每個較低信任區域，每個管理埠均不可觸及；只允許來自管理區域的已記錄流量。掃描**僅**針對小組自身的實驗室進行，且當來源為模擬器裝置時，結果以模擬模式擷取 | | |

  EN: | TC-SEC-28 | Plaintext management protocols are absent | `show ip ssh` and `show line vty` on network devices; `sshd -T` on Linux; check that Telnet is disabled and that WinRM 5985 is not used for real work | SSH transport enabled and Telnet disabled on network devices; SSH key-only on Linux; WinRM HTTPS 5986 used by Ansible; any exception is documented as a build-time step that was later closed | | |
  ZH: | TC-SEC-28 | 不存在明文管理協定 | 網路裝置上執行 `show ip ssh` 及 `show line vty`；Linux 上執行 `sshd -T`；檢查 Telnet 已停用且實際工作不使用 WinRM 5985 | 網路裝置上 SSH 傳輸已啟用而 Telnet 已停用；Linux 上僅使用 SSH 金鑰；Ansible 使用 WinRM HTTPS 5986；任何例外均記錄為其後已結案的建構階段步驟 | | |

  EN: ### 13.6 Performance and Capacity Testing
  ZH: ### 13.6 效能與容量測試
L748 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L749 DIFF
  EN: Performance testing in this project measures the **estate that was actually built** — a virtualised lab on a single hypervisor host with no physical hardware — so its purpose is to demonstrate a defensible measurement method and to expose resource limits, not to publish production capacity figures. Every number in this section is labelled accordingly, and the report states plainly that **no test here can measure real forwarding hardware, real line rate, real cabling or real power behaviour**, because none of those exist in the design.
  ZH: 本項目的效能測試所量測的是**實際建成的那個環境**——單一虛擬化平台主機上的虛擬化實驗室，沒有任何實體硬體——因此其目的是展示一套站得住腳的量測方法並揭露資源限制，而非發佈生產容量數字。本節中每個數字均如此標註，且報告明確聲明**此處沒有任何測試能量測真實的轉發硬體、真實的線速、真實的佈線或真實的電力行為**，因為設計中這些都不存在。

L792 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L793 DIFF
  EN: **What is explicitly outside the gate.** Autonomous remediation is not tested and not claimed: the design deliberately excludes any write path, on safety and academic-integrity grounds, because an unsupervised agent writing to a router or a domain controller would make the project's verification results irreproducible. Model hosting, fine-tuning, GPU inference and predictive analytics with trained models are likewise out of scope and appear only as future work. The standard aligned to this project does not require any of them — the WSOS contains no AI content at all — so their absence cannot be presented as a shortfall against a standard, only as declared scope of a proof of concept.
  ZH: **明確不在閘門之內的事項。** 自主修復不予測試亦不予聲稱：基於安全及學術誠信理由，設計刻意排除任何寫入路徑，因為一個無人監督的代理若寫入路由器或網域控制站，將使本項目的驗證結果無法重現。模型託管、微調、GPU 推論及使用已訓練模型的預測分析同樣不在範圍內，僅作為未來工作出現。本項目所對齊的標準並不要求其中任何一項——WSOS 完全不含 AI 內容——因此它們的缺席不能作為相對於某項標準的不足來呈現，只能作為概念驗證的已聲明範圍。

L829 DIFF
  EN: **Acceptance of the documentation itself.** Documentation is accepted when: every section listed in the deliverable set exists and is complete; every figure and table is captioned and referenced in the text; every screenshot has a caption of the prescribed form and a row in the evidence index; the findings register contains every deviation discovered, including those that make the design look less complete; the version-deviation subsection exists and lists each difference between a pinned version and the running system with its impact; and the sign-off sheet carries a date and a signature.
  ZH: **文件本身的驗收。** 文件在下列情況獲得驗收：交付物清單中所列的每個章節均存在且完整；每個圖及每個表均有說明並在正文中被引用；每張截圖均具有規定形式的說明及證據索引中的一列；發現登記冊載有所有已發現的偏差，包括那些使設計看起來較不完整的偏差；版本偏差小節存在並列出鎖定版本與實際運行系統之間的每一項差異及其影響；且簽核表載有日期及簽名。

L877 DIFF
  EN: **Result categories.** Each criterion is recorded as one of three outcomes, and the third category must never be hidden: **Met** (demonstrated with evidence); **Met with declared limitation** (demonstrated, with the limitation stated and indexed); **Not met — reported as a finding** (recorded in the findings register with its cause and its impact). Presenting a limitation honestly is a stronger academic result than reporting a false pass, and the baseline makes this an explicit project rule.
  ZH: **結果類別。** 每項準則記錄為三種結果之一，而第三類絕不得隱藏：**達成**（有證據地展示）；**達成並附已聲明限制**（已展示，限制已聲明並索引）；**未達成——作為發現報告**（連同其原因及影響記錄於發現登記冊）。誠實呈現一項限制是比報告虛假通過更強的學術成果，而基準使這成為本項目的明確規則。

L885 DIFF
  EN: | **Log** | A defect is raised the moment a test fails or an unexpected behaviour is observed, with the test case ID, the expected result, the observed result, the exact command, and the environment state (snapshot name, running VMs, PT file) | Whoever observed it | Issue labelled `type:defect`, plus a findings-register row if the behaviour was unexpected rather than a simple error |
  ZH: | **記錄** | 測試一失敗或觀察到意外行為時即提出缺陷，載有測試案例 ID、預期結果、觀察到的結果、確切命令，以及環境狀態（快照名稱、運行中的 VM、PT 檔案） | 觀察到的人 | 標籤為 `type:defect` 的問題，若該行為屬意外而非單純錯誤，另加一列發現登記冊 |

  ZH: | **分類** | 指派嚴重性、優先級及受影響線；該缺陷可被接受、延後至指名的稍後窗口，或被以「設計如此」為由拒絕並記錄原因 | 受影響線的擁有人加一名審查者；由整合擁有人主持 | 問題上的嚴重性及優先級欄位；分類決定及理由 |

L888 DIFF
  EN: | **Fix** | The fix is applied through the normal path — configuration text in Git, a playbook change, or a script change — never by hand-editing a running system and leaving the change unrecorded | The assignee | Commit referencing the defect ID |
  ZH: | **修正** | 修正是透過正常路徑套用——Git 中的配置文字、playbook 變更或腳本變更——絕不透過手動編輯運行中的系統並使該變更不被記錄 | 受派人 | 引用該缺陷 ID 的提交 |
L889 DIFF
  EN: | **Re-test** | The originating test case is re-executed, **and** the whole-estate verification playbook is re-run to catch regressions the fix introduced | The assignee, with a second member observing for Critical and High defects | Re-executed test record with the new result |
  ZH: | **重測** | 重新執行肇始的測試案例，**並**重跑整個環境的驗證 playbook 以捕捉該修正引入的迴歸 | 受派人，並有一名第二成員就 Critical 及 High 缺陷進行觀察 | 載有新結果的重新執行測試記錄 |
L890 DIFF
  EN: | **Close** | The defect closes only when the re-test passes and the evidence is indexed. A defect closed without a re-execution is reopened automatically at the next gate review | Integration owner | Closure with the re-test evidence reference |
  ZH: | **結案** | 缺陷僅在重測通過且證據已索引時結案。未經重新執行而結案的缺陷，在下一次閘門審查中自動重開 | 整合擁有人 | 附重測證據參考的結案 |
L891 DIFF
  EN: | **Escalate** | A defect in the **must** band that cannot be fixed within its window escalates to the change-control board within 24 hours, because the must band is non-negotiable and the response is schedule re-planning, not scope reduction | Project lead | Change record referencing the defect |
  ZH: | **上呈** | **必須**帶中無法在其窗口內修正的缺陷，於 24 小時內上呈至變更控制委員會，因為「必須」帶不可協商，且應對方式是重新規劃進度，而非縮減範圍 | 項目負責人 | 引用該缺陷的變更記錄 |

  ZH: 
L902 DIFF
  EN: **Priority versus severity.** Severity describes the technical impact; priority describes the order of work. A High-severity defect on the critical path takes priority over a Critical-severity defect that is off it and has a known workaround — but a Critical defect is never deferred past the phase gate regardless of priority, because the must band is non-negotiable.
  ZH: **優先級與嚴重性。** 嚴重性描述技術影響；優先級描述工作順序。關鍵路徑上一項 High 嚴重性缺陷，優先於一項不在關鍵路徑上且已有已知替代方案的 Critical 嚴重性缺陷——但無論優先級如何，Critical 缺陷絕不延後至階段閘門之後，因為「必須」帶不可協商。
L903 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L904 DIFF
  EN: **Rules that make defect management evidence rather than administration.**
  ZH: **使缺陷管理成為證據而非行政工作的規則。**
L905 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L906 DIFF
  EN: 1. A defect is never closed by deleting or weakening the test that found it. If the test was wrong, the test is corrected through change control, with the reason recorded.
  ZH: 1. 缺陷絕不透過刪除或弱化發現它的測試而結案。若該測試有錯，則透過變更控制修正該測試並記錄原因。
L907 DIFF
  EN: 2. Every defect that reveals an incorrect assumption is copied into the findings register and the assumptions log, and the risk register is reviewed for a matching risk.
  ZH: 2. 每一項揭示錯誤假設的缺陷均複製至發現登記冊及假設日誌，並檢視風險登記冊是否有對應的風險。
L908 DIFF
  EN: 3. Every defect fixed after the technology freeze requires a change record, so the freeze remains meaningful rather than decorative.
  ZH: 3. 技術凍結後修正的每一項缺陷均須有變更記錄，使該凍結保持有意義而非僅作裝飾。
L909 DIFF
  EN: 4. The defect list is reviewed at every weekly gate, and the residual defect list is part of the acceptance record — a project with three known Medium defects that are documented and understood is in better shape than one that claims none.
  ZH: 4. 缺陷清單在每次每週閘門中檢視，而餘下缺陷清單是驗收記錄的一部分——一個有三項已知、已記錄並已理解的 Medium 缺陷的項目，狀況優於一個聲稱毫無缺陷的項目。

L929 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L930 DIFF
  EN: **Attribution discipline.** Two facts about these six categories must not be blended together. First, the **screen-capture requirement comes from the brief**, not from the standard aligned to this project: the WSOS 2026 page contains no requirement for screen captures, and the strings `screen`, `capture`, `screenshot` and `evidence` occur **zero times** on it. Second, the standard **does** require documentation — three times, including the line quoted in section 13.8 about preparing a technical document for agreement and sign-off. This proposal therefore presents the evidence set as a **brief requirement** and the technical document as a **brief and standard requirement**, and it does not claim that the standard asks for screenshots.
  ZH: **歸屬紀律。** 關於這六個類別的兩項事實絕不可混為一談。第一，**螢幕擷取要求來自簡介**，而非來自本項目所對齊的標準：WSOS 2026 頁面不含任何螢幕擷取要求，而 `screen`、`capture`、`screenshot` 及 `evidence` 等字串在該頁出現**零次**。第二，該標準**確實**要求文件——出現三次，包括第 13.8 節所引用關於為達成共識及簽核而編製技術文件的那一行。因此本提案將證據集呈現為**簡介要求**，將技術文件呈現為**簡介及標準要求**，且不聲稱該標準要求截圖。

  EN: ### 14.3 Formatting and Writing Standards
  ZH: ### 14.3 格式及寫作標準
L980 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L981 DIFF
  EN: | Element | Rule | Rationale |
  ZH: | 元素 | 規則 | 理由 |
L982 SAME
  EN: |---|---|---|
  ZH: |---|---|---|
L983 DIFF
  EN: | **Heading levels** | The report uses one top level for the document, numbered sections beneath it, then numbered subsections, then a fourth level only where a table needs its own grouping. No deeper nesting | Deeper nesting makes a technical report unreadable and makes cross-references fragile |
  ZH: | **標題層級** | 報告對文件使用一個頂層，其下為編號節，再下為編號小節，僅在表格需要自身分組處使用第四層。不作更深的嵌套 | 更深的嵌套使技術報告不可讀，並使交叉引用變得脆弱 |
L984 DIFF
  EN: | **Numbering** | Sections, subsections and sub-subsections are numbered contiguously; every table, figure and appendix is numbered; cross-references always cite a number, never "above" or "below" | A numbered target survives editing; a positional reference does not |
  ZH: | **編號** | 節、小節及次小節連續編號；每個表、圖及附錄均編號；交叉引用一律引用編號，絕不使用「上文」或「下文」 | 編號目標能在編輯後存續；位置式引用則否 |
L985 DIFF
  EN: | **Table convention** | Every table has a caption, a number, a source line if it reproduces another document, and a first column that is an identifier wherever the rows are citable (a test ID, a rule ID, a host name) | Identifiers make the artefact auditable: a rule or a test can be cited from the report, the test record and the issue tracker with the same string |
  ZH: | **表格慣例** | 每個表均有說明、編號、在轉載另一文件時的來源行，以及在列可被引用時（測試 ID、規則 ID、主機名稱）以識別碼作為第一欄 | 識別碼使產物可稽核：一條規則或一項測試可從報告、測試記錄及問題追蹤器以同一字串引用 |
L986 DIFF
  EN: | **Figure captioning** | The fixed template is `Figure {n}: {what} — {host} — {command or action} — {date}` | The caption carries the four things a reader needs to judge the evidence: what, where, how, when |
  ZH: | **圖的說明** | 固定範本為 `Figure {n}: {what} — {host} — {command or action} — {date}` | 說明載有讀者評判該證據所需的四件事：什麼、何處、如何、何時 |
L987 DIFF
  EN: | **Code and command blocks** | Every fenced block carries a language tag: `powershell` for PowerShell, `bash` for shell, `yaml` for playbooks and structured configuration, `text` for `show` output, trees, diagrams and plain command transcripts, `json` for machine-readable results | Untagged blocks defeat syntax highlighting and make the type of content ambiguous |
  ZH: | **程式碼及命令區塊** | 每個圍欄區塊均帶語言標籤：PowerShell 用 `powershell`，shell 用 `bash`，playbook 及結構化配置用 `yaml`，`show` 輸出、樹狀圖、示意圖及純命令逐字稿用 `text`，機器可讀結果用 `json` | 無標籤的區塊會使語法高亮失效，並使內容類型含糊 |
L988 DIFF
  EN: | **Command convention** | Commands appear verbatim and executable, with the host they run on stated and prompts omitted so that a reader can copy them | A command that has been paraphrased for readability can no longer be re-run, which destroys its evidential value |
  ZH: | **命令慣例** | 命令以逐字且可執行的形式出現，並聲明其所運行於的主機，同時省略提示符，使讀者可以複製 | 為可讀性而改寫過的命令無法再被重跑，這摧毀了其證據價值 |
L989 DIFF
  EN: | **Terminology consistency** | The canonical strings of the interface contract are used everywhere, in the same casing: `vpnz-dc01` is never written `DC01`, `VP-HQ-CORE-01` is never written `HQ-Core-1`, and a VLAN is named exactly as in the VLAN plan | Re-capitalisation is the most common way four writers silently diverge; the contract forbids it explicitly |
  ZH: | **術語一致性** | 介面契約的權威字串在所有地方以相同大小寫使用：`vpnz-dc01` 絕不寫作 `DC01`，`VP-HQ-CORE-01` 絕不寫作 `HQ-Core-1`，而 VLAN 的名稱與 VLAN 計劃完全一致 | 重新調整大小寫是四名撰寫者悄悄分歧的最常見方式；契約明確禁止 |
L990 DIFF
  EN: | **One technology, one name** | A technology is named once and used consistently thereafter; where a vendor name and a protocol name differ, the protocol is used in protocol discussions and the vendor name in product discussions | Prevents the reader from suspecting two technologies where there is one |
  ZH: | **一項技術一個名稱** | 一項技術只命名一次，其後一致使用；當廠商名稱與協定名稱不同時，協定討論中使用協定名，產品討論中使用廠商名 | 防止讀者在只有一項技術之處懷疑有兩項 |
L991 DIFF
  EN: | **Abbreviation discipline** | Every abbreviation is expanded in full at first use, with the abbreviation in brackets, and is used thereafter; a glossary is maintained. Acronyms that the standard itself names are quoted exactly as the standard prints them, including its own unusual forms | A report with unexplained abbreviations is unusable by an assessor who is not from that specialism, and mis-quoting a standard's own wording is a citation error |
  ZH: | **縮寫紀律** | 每個縮寫在首次使用時全寫展開、縮寫置於括號內，其後沿用；並維護一份詞彙表。標準本身所指名的縮寫依標準的印法逐字引用，包括其自身不尋常的形式 | 附有未解釋縮寫的報告，對非該專業領域的評核者而言無法使用；而誤引標準自身的措辭屬引用錯誤 |
L992 DIFF
  EN: | **Units and numerals** | Units are always given, never implied; bit rates are distinguished from byte rates; addresses always carry a mask or prefix length where the mask matters | "500 Mb" is ambiguous; in an evidence-based report it is a defect |
  ZH: | **單位及數字** | 一律給出單位，絕不暗示；位元率與位元組率予以區分；遮罩重要之處，位址一律附遮罩或前綴長度 | 「500 Mb」含義不明；在以證據為本的報告中這是一項缺陷 |
L993 DIFF
  EN: | **Voice** | Third person, present or past tense as appropriate for design versus execution; no first-person narration and no marketing adjectives | An assessor must be able to separate what was designed, what was built and what was measured |
  ZH: | **語氣** | 第三人稱，就設計與執行之別分別使用現在式或過去式；不使用第一人稱敘述，也不使用行銷形容詞 | 評核者必須能區分什麼是所設計的、什麼是已建成的、什麼是所量測的 |
L994 DIFF
  EN: | **Honesty phrasing** | Claims are worded to match their evidence: "verified by the transcript in Appendix G" where evidence exists, and "not implemented — see the declared exclusions" where it does not. Words such as *certified*, *compliant*, *production* and *real-world throughput* are used only where evidence supports them, and the final proof-read evidences or removes every occurrence | The design is aligned with selected controls and is **not** certified against them; this distinction is a project rule, not a preference |
  ZH: | **誠實措辭** | 主張的措辭與其證據相符：有證據處寫「已由 Appendix G 的逐字稿驗證」，無證據處寫「未實作——見已聲明的排除項目」。*certified*、*compliant*、*production* 及 *real-world throughput* 等詞僅在有證據支持時使用，而最後一遍校對須為每一處出現提供證據或予以刪除 | 本設計與所選控制項目對齊，而**並非**就該等項目獲得認證；這一區別是本項目的規則，而非偏好 |
L995 DIFF
  EN: | **Date format** | ISO 8601 (`2026-10-06`) everywhere, in captions, filenames, indices and the change log | Sortable, unambiguous, and consistent with the evidence file-naming convention |
  ZH: | **日期格式** | 各處均使用 ISO 8601（`2026-10-06`），包括說明、檔案名稱、索引及變更日誌 | 可排序、無歧義，並與證據檔案命名慣例一致 |

  EN: ### 14.4 Screenshot and Evidence Standards
  ZH: ### 14.4 截圖及證據標準
L998 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L999 DIFF
  EN: **Capture.** Screenshots are taken of the whole window that carries the meaning, including the host name and the command that produced the output, at native resolution without scaling. Where a state is transient — a failover in progress, a quota being exceeded, a denial appearing in a live log — the capture is taken during the event, and wherever possible the same event is also recorded as a text transcript so that the two corroborate each other. Long terminal output is captured in overlapping scrolls rather than in one illegible frame, and the overlap is stated in the caption.
  ZH: **擷取。** 截圖擷取承載意義的整個視窗，包括主機名稱及產生該輸出的命令，以原生解析度、不縮放。當某狀態屬短暫（進行中的故障轉移、正在被超出配額、即時日誌中出現的拒絕）時，擷取在事件期間進行，並盡可能將同一事件同時記錄為文字逐字稿，使兩者互相佐證。長終端輸出以重疊捲動方式擷取，而非放在一個無法辨讀的畫面中，且該重疊在說明中聲明。

  ZH: **確切的檔案命名慣例。** 基準固定如下：

L1013 DIFF
  EN: The three fields are deliberately chosen so that a filename answers the three questions an assessor asks: **when** (`date`, ISO 8601), **where** (`host`, using the canonical hostname or device name from the interface contract) and **what** (`what`, a short lower-case hyphenated description). Filenames contain no spaces, no non-ASCII characters and no personal names.
  ZH: 這三個欄位是刻意選定的，使檔案名稱能回答評核者所問的三個問題：**何時**（`date`，ISO 8601）、**何處**（`host`，使用介面契約中的權威主機名稱或裝置名稱）以及**什麼**（`what`，一段簡短的小寫連字號描述）。檔案名稱不含空格、不含非 ASCII 字元，也不含人名。
L1014 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L1015 DIFF
  EN: **Cropping and annotation.** Cropping is allowed only to remove unrelated screen area — never to remove context a reader needs in order to judge the evidence, and never to remove a warning, an error or a value that is inconvenient. On the contrary: where a name-mismatch warning, a self-signed certificate warning or a version deviation is visible, the capture must **include** it and the caption must explain it. Annotation is by numbered callout with a short legend, using a single colour, and every callout number in the image appears in the caption. Annotations are added with an offline tool and the annotated file replaces the raw capture in `07-evidence\`; the raw capture is kept in the same directory with a `-raw` suffix where the annotation covers anything material.
  ZH: **裁剪及標註。** 裁剪僅允許用於移除無關的螢幕區域——絕不可移除讀者評判該證據所需的脈絡，也絕不可移除一則警告、一個錯誤或不方便的數值。恰恰相反：當名稱不符警告、自簽憑證警告或版本偏差可見時，擷取**必須包含**它，而說明必須解釋它。標註採編號標記加簡短圖例、使用單一顏色，且圖中每個標記編號均出現於說明中。標註以離線工具加上，註記後的檔案取代 `07-evidence\` 中的原始擷取；若標註遮蓋任何重要內容，原始擷取以 `-raw` 後綴保留於同一目錄。
L1016 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L1017 DIFF
  EN: **Indexing and storage.** All captures live in `07-evidence\` and every one has a row in the master evidence index of section 14.9. Captures are staged first in `C:\FYP\evidence\` on the Windows host on which they were taken, and are transferred to `07-evidence\` at the weekly documentation session together with their index rows. Captures used inside the report are referenced by their index identifier, and the index row carries the report section and figure number, so the report can be checked against the evidence in both directions.
  ZH: **索引及儲存。** 所有擷取均存放於 `07-evidence\`，且每一張在第 14.9 節的主證據索引中均有一列。擷取先暫存於其被擷取所在 Windows 主機的 `C:\FYP\evidence\`，並在每週文件時段連同其索引列一併移轉至 `07-evidence\`。報告中使用的擷取以其索引識別碼引用，而索引列載有報告章節及圖號，使報告可與證據雙向核查。
L1018 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L1019 DIFF
  EN: **Why an unindexed screenshot is worthless as evidence.** A screenshot that is not named to the convention and not indexed fails in three ways at once. It is **unfindable**: nobody can locate the one image among hundreds that supports a specific claim, so the claim becomes unverifiable in practice even though the image exists. It is **undated and unattributed**: without the naming fields there is no reliable link to a test execution, a host, a moment or a performer, so an assessor cannot tell whether it was taken during the build, during a test, or after the fact. And it is **unlinkable**: it cannot be referenced by the traceability matrix, so a technology or an objective that depends on it appears to have no evidence at all. The result is the worst of both worlds — the work was done, the proof exists, and the project still cannot demonstrate it. Indexing is therefore treated as part of taking the screenshot rather than as a later chore: a capture with no index row is, by project rule, not yet evidence.
  ZH: **為何未經索引的截圖作為證據毫無價值。** 未依慣例命名且未索引的截圖同時在三方面失敗。它**無法找到**：沒有人能在數百張圖片中找出支持某一特定主張的那一張，因此即使該圖片存在，該主張在實務上仍無法驗證。它**無日期且無歸屬**：缺乏命名欄位就沒有與某次測試執行、某部主機、某個時刻或某位執行者的可靠連結，因此評核者無從分辨它是在建構期間、測試期間還是事後擷取。而且它**無法連結**：它不能被追溯矩陣引用，因此依賴它的某項技術或目標看起來完全沒有證據。結果是兩種情況中最壞的一種——工作做了、證明存在，而項目仍然無法展示它。因此索引被視為截圖工作的一部分，而非日後的雜務：依項目規則，沒有索引列的擷取還不算證據。

  ZH:

  EN: ### 14.6 Findings Register
  ZH: ### 14.6 發現登記冊
L1055 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L1056 DIFF
  EN: The findings register records what the project **learnt**, which is a different thing from what it **did**. It captures unexpected behaviour, deviations from the design, workarounds, experiment results, negative results and lessons learned, and it is a required deliverable under the brief's "findings" category. Every finding has an identifier, an owner and a disposition, and a finding is never deleted even when it is resolved — a resolved finding with a documented cause is the most useful row in the register.
  ZH: 發現登記冊記錄本項目**學到**了什麼，這與它**做了**什麼是不同的事情。它擷取意外行為、與設計的偏差、替代方案、實驗結果、負面結果及經驗教訓，並且是簡介中「發現」類別所要求的交付物。每項發現均有一個識別碼、一名擁有人及一項處置，而發現即使已解決也絕不刪除——一項附有已記錄原因的已解決發現，是登記冊中最有用的列。

L1098 DIFF
  EN: ### 14.7 Reference Management
  ZH: ### 14.7 參考文獻管理
L1099 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L1100 DIFF
  EN: Every technical claim in this project resolves to a reference of one of four kinds: a **standard or framework** (the WorldSkills Occupational Standards, or an RFC), **vendor or platform documentation** for the exact version installed, the project's **own experiment** with a test-case identifier, or the **project brief** itself.
  ZH: 本項目中的每項技術主張均解析至四類參考文獻之一：**標準或框架**（WorldSkills 職業標準，或一份 RFC）、就所安裝確切版本而言的**廠商或平台文件**、附測試案例識別碼的本項目**自身實驗**，或**項目簡介**本身。
L1101 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L1102 DIFF
  EN: **Recording.** Each citation is a row in a single register containing: a citation key; the author or issuing body; the title as printed; the edition, version or date; the URL **only where the source was genuinely retrieved and that URL is available in the project's own research pack or was copied from the browser at the moment of access**; the access date; which member consulted it; and which report sections cite it. The consulted artefact is archived under `08-references\`, one file per citation, named with the citation key so that the register row and the archived file always agree. Where a source is printed or offline, the register records where it is held instead of a URL.
  ZH: **記錄。** 每項引用是單一登記冊中的一列，載有：引用鍵；作者或發布機構；依印法的標題；版次、版本或日期；URL**僅在該來源確實被取得，且該 URL 存在於本項目自身的研究包中，或在存取當刻從瀏覽器複製**；存取日期；查閱該來源的成員；以及引用它的報告章節。所查閱的產物歸檔於 `08-references\`，每項引用一個檔案，以引用鍵命名，使登記冊列與歸檔檔案永遠一致。當來源為印刷品或離線時，登記冊記錄其存放位置而非 URL。
L1103 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L1104 DIFF
  EN: **Citation style.** In-text citations give the issuing body and a short form of the document title, with the full entry in the reference list; direct quotations are reproduced verbatim inside quotation marks, preserving the source's own spelling and punctuation, including its typographical quirks, and the section or line the quotation comes from is stated. Where a quotation contains an error in the source, the quotation is reproduced as printed rather than silently corrected, because a corrected quotation is no longer a quotation. Where a protocol specification is cited, the reference names the document and its number — for the addressing conventions used in this design the applicable documents are RFC 1918 for private address space, RFC 5737 for the documentation address blocks, RFC 2606 for reserved name suffixes and RFC 4193, which is cited only to state that this project is IPv4-only and that IPv6 is out of scope. No clause number is invented anywhere: where an edition of a specification was consulted for background rather than quoted, the register marks it as consulted but not quoted.
  ZH: **引用風格。** 文內引用給出發布機構及文件標題的簡短形式，完整條目置於參考文獻清單；直接引文在引號內逐字重現，保留來源自身的拼寫及標點，包括其排版上的奇特之處，並聲明該引文所來自的節或行。當引文含有來源本身的錯誤時，該引文依印法重現而非悄悄更正，因為更正過的引文已不再是引文。當引用某項協定規格時，參考文獻指明該文件及其編號——就本設計所用的定址慣例而言，適用文件為私有位址空間的 RFC 1918、文件專用位址區塊的 RFC 5737、保留名稱後綴的 RFC 2606，以及 RFC 4193（僅引以聲明本項目僅使用 IPv4 且 IPv6 不在範圍內）。任何地方均不發明條款編號：當某版規格僅為背景而查閱而非引用時，登記冊標示其為已查閱但未引用。
L1105 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L1106 DIFF
  EN: **The rule that every reference be genuinely consulted.** Four rules apply, and they are contract rules rather than style advice:
  ZH: **每一項參考文獻均須確實查閱過的規則。** 適用四項規則，而它們是契約規則而非風格建議：
L1107 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L1108 DIFF
  EN: 1. **No URL may be written from memory.** Every URL in the reference list is either present in the project's own verified research pack or was copied from the browser at the time of access, and the archived copy is the proof.
  ZH: 1. **不得憑記憶寫出任何 URL。** 參考文獻清單中的每個 URL 不是存在於本項目自身已核實的研究包中，就是在存取當刻從瀏覽器複製，而歸檔副本即為證明。
L1109 DIFF
  EN: 2. **A source that could not be read is cited as cited-by.** If a document is known only through another document that quotes it, the reference says so explicitly rather than presenting it as if it had been read.
  ZH: 2. **無法閱讀的來源以「轉引」方式引用。** 若某文件僅透過另一份引用它的文件而得知，參考文獻明確如此聲明，而非呈現得像是已讀過。
L1110 DIFF
  EN: 3. **A version number is cited only together with the command output that produced it.** A version claim without its producing command is not a reference, it is an assertion.
  ZH: 3. **版本號僅連同產生它的命令輸出一起引用。** 沒有產生命令的版本主張不是參考文獻，而是一項斷言。
L1111 DIFF
  EN: 4. **Nothing may be cited for a fact it does not contain.** This rule matters most where the temptation is greatest: the WSOS 2026 page is never cited for the twelve-week duration, for screen captures, or for any technology string that does not occur on it — it is not cited for FSRM, iSCSI, RRAS, IKEv2, Keepalived, nftables, WireGuard, RSTP, ASA, Agentic AI, OpenClaw or MCP, because those strings occur zero times. Where a bridge is used instead, the standard's line is quoted and the inference is then stated as an inference.
  ZH: 4. **不得就某項事實引用不含該事實的來源。** 此規則在誘惑最大之處最為重要：WSOS 2026 頁面絕不被引以支持十二週的時長、螢幕擷取，或該頁上不存在的任何技術字串——它不被引以支持 FSRM、iSCSI、RRAS、IKEv2、Keepalived、nftables、WireGuard、RSTP、ASA、Agentic AI、OpenClaw 或 MCP，因為這些字串出現零次。若改以橋接方式處理，則引用標準的那一行，然後將該推論聲明為推論。
L1112 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L1113 DIFF
  EN: ### 14.8 Version Control for Documentation
  ZH: ### 14.8 文件的版本控制
L1114 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L1115 DIFF
  EN: Documentation is versioned in **the same repository and by the same mechanism** as configuration and code. The justification is straightforward: the value of a design document is that it describes a specific state of a specific system. If the document and the system have separate histories, the document silently becomes a description of a system that no longer exists.
  ZH: 文件以**同一個儲存庫並透過相同機制**與配置及程式碼一同作版本控制。理由很直接：設計文件的價值在於它描述某個特定系統的某個特定狀態。若文件與系統各有獨立的歷史，文件便悄悄變成對一個已不復存在之系統的描述。
L1116 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L1117 DIFF
  EN: | Practice | Rule |
  ZH: | 實務 | 規則 |
L1118 SAME
  EN: |---|---|
  ZH: |---|---|
L1119 DIFF
  EN: | Repository | One repository at `C:\FYP` for design, build artefacts, automation, verification, evidence index and report chapters |
  ZH: | 儲存庫 | 在 `C:\FYP` 設置一個儲存庫，容納設計、建構產物、自動化、驗證、證據索引及報告章節 |
L1120 DIFF
  EN: | Branching | Branch-per-task, named for the work package or issue; documentation branches are named the same way as configuration branches so that a change to a system and the change to its description travel together |
  ZH: | 分支 | 每個任務一個分支，以工作包或問題命名；文件分支與配置分支以相同方式命名，使對系統的變更與對其描述的變更一同前進 |
L1121 DIFF
  EN: | Commits | One logical change per commit; the commit message states the change and ends with the issue ID, so `git log` and the issue tracker reconstruct each other |
  ZH: | 提交 | 每次提交一項邏輯變更；提交訊息聲明該變更並以問題 ID 結尾，使 `git log` 與問題追蹤器能互相重建 |
L1122 DIFF
  EN: | Peer review | Every merge into the main line is reviewed by a second member and the review is recorded in the meeting minutes. This applies to documents as well as playbooks, because the scenario's due-diligence requirement asks for change applied through *reviewed* configuration |
  ZH: | 同儕審查 | 每次合併至主線均由第二名成員審查，且該審查記錄於會議紀錄。這適用於文件亦適用於 playbook，因為情境的盡職審查要求變更須透過*經審查*的配置套用 |
L1123 DIFF
  EN: | Review of document changes | A documentation change is reviewed for three things: technical accuracy, consistency with the interface contract, and whether the change affects a claim made elsewhere in the document set — for example a claim in a chapter and the result in the test report |
  ZH: | 文件變更的審查 | 文件變更就三件事受審查：技術準確性、與介面契約的一致性，以及該變更是否影響文件集中他處所作的一項主張——例如某章節中的主張與測試報告中的結果 |
L1124 DIFF
  EN: | Large or binary artefacts | Screenshots, `.pkt` files and PDF exports are committed because they are evidence; the rule is that a reviewed artefact is committed, not merely stored |
  ZH: | 大型或二進位產物 | 截圖、`.pkt` 檔案及 PDF 匯出均予提交，因為它們是證據；規則是已審查的產物須被提交，而非僅被儲存 |
L1125 DIFF
  EN: | Secrets | Keys, `pki/` material, `wg0.conf`, certificates with private keys and plaintext passwords are never committed. A pre-tag sweep for those patterns, and for the project's banned strings, runs before every release tag, and its output is committed as evidence |
  ZH: | 機密 | 金鑰、`pki/` 材料、`wg0.conf`、含私鑰的憑證及明文密碼絕不提交。在每次發行標籤之前，會針對該等模式及本項目的禁用字串執行標籤前清查，其輸出作為證據提交 |
L1126 DIFF
  EN: | Tags | Milestone tags from `v0.1` through `v1.0`; `v1.0` is the submitted state and is tagged only after the week-12 consistency sweep passes |
  ZH: | 標籤 | 里程碑標籤自 `v0.1` 至 `v1.0`；`v1.0` 是所提交的狀態，且僅在第 12 週的一致性清查通過後才標記 |
L1127 DIFF
  EN: | The word "final" | Used only for a revision that has passed that sweep, so that the word keeps its meaning |
  ZH: | 「final」一詞 | 僅用於已通過該清查的修訂版本，使該詞保持其含義 |
L1128 DIFF
  EN: | Off-host copies | The repository is mirrored to a second medium so that no member's machine failure can lose the project, and no member is the sole holder of any artefact |
  ZH: | 離主機副本 | 儲存庫鏡像至第二種媒體，使任何成員的機器故障都無法使項目丟失，且沒有任何成員是任何產物的唯一持有人 |
L1129 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L1130 DIFF
  EN: **Where documentation is generated by automation.** Diagrams, IP and VLAN plan exports and parts of the inventory are produced from configuration and committed together with the command that generated them. Where automation generates a document, the generator is itself versioned, and the generated document records the generator's version and the run timestamp, so that a discrepancy between the document and the system can be traced to a run rather than to a person.
  ZH: **文件由自動化產生的情況。** 圖表、IP 及 VLAN 計劃匯出，以及部分清單，均由配置產生並連同產生它們的命令一併提交。當自動化產生文件時，產生器本身亦受版本控制，而所產生的文件記錄產生器的版本及執行時間戳，使文件與系統之間的差異可追溯至某次執行，而非某個人。

  EN: **Index rules.** Every evidence file has exactly one row; every row names exactly one capturer; an evidence ID is never reused even when the underlying file is replaced (a replacement takes a new ID and the old row records the supersession); the index is committed to the same repository as the images; and the report cites evidence by index ID rather than by filename, so that moving a file does not break the report.

  ZH: ## 15. 風險管理
L1162 SAME
  EN:

L1163 DIFF
  EN: ### 15.1 Risk Management Approach
  ZH: ### 15.1 風險管理方針
L1164 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L1165 DIFF
  EN: Risk management in this project is deliberately lightweight in process and rigorous in record. The method has five elements.
  ZH: 本項目的風險管理在程序上刻意精簡，在記錄上則嚴謹。該方法有五個要素。
L1166 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L1167 DIFF
  EN: **1. A single register, reviewed weekly.** One risk register is maintained in the repository. It is reviewed at every weekly gate review, and every row has exactly one owner. The register is never rewritten from scratch: rows are updated, closed with a reason, or added, so that the history of the project's risk picture stays visible.
  ZH: **1. 單一登記冊，每週審查。** 儲存庫中維護一份風險登記冊。它在每次每週閘門審查中受檢視，且每列恰好有一名擁有人。登記冊絕不從頭重寫：列被更新、附理由結案或新增，使本項目風險圖景的歷史保持可見。
L1168 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L1169 DIFF
  EN: **2. Structured identification.** Risks are identified from five sources: the design baseline's own risk material and its assumption and limitation tables; each phase gate review (what nearly went wrong this week); each defect and finding, since a defect is evidence that a risk was under-estimated; a deliberate re-review at three fixed points (after the environment build, after the perimeter is live, and before the technology freeze); and the standard's own competency areas viewed as failure modes — failure to plan, failure to communicate between colleagues, failure to document completion, failure to test and re-test.
  ZH: **2. 結構化識別。** 風險自五個來源識別：設計基準自身的風險材料及其假設與限制表；每次階段閘門審查（本週差點出什麼問題）；每項缺陷及發現，因為缺陷是某項風險被低估的證據；在三個固定時點（環境建構之後、周邊上線之後，以及技術凍結之前）的刻意重新檢視；以及將標準自身的職能領域視為失敗模式——未能規劃、未能與同事溝通、未能記錄完成情況、未能測試並重新測試。
L1170 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L1171 DIFF
  EN: **3. Scoring scales.** Likelihood and impact are each scored on a four-point scale, severity is their product expressed as a band, and the band determines the response obligation. The project uses its own four-point scale rather than the design baseline's High/Medium/Low wording, so that likelihood and impact can be reasoned about separately; the mapping from the baseline's own risk table to this register is given at the end of section 15.2.
  ZH: **3. 評分尺度。** 可能性與影響各以四點尺度評分，嚴重性是二者之乘積並以帶表示，而該帶決定應對義務。本項目使用自身的四點尺度而非設計基準的 High／Medium／Low 措辭，使可能性與影響可分別推理；由基準自身風險表至本登記冊的對應列於第 15.2 節末。
L1172 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L1173 DIFF
  EN: | Score | Likelihood | Impact | Severity band (product) | Response obligation |
  ZH: | 分數 | 可能性 | 影響 | 嚴重性帶（乘積） | 應對義務 |
L1174 SAME
  EN: |---|---|---|---|---|

L1175 DIFF
  EN: | **4** | Very likely — expected to occur on current evidence | Severe — a must-band capability, an acceptance criterion or the submission itself is lost | **Critical (12–16)**: immediate action with a named owner and a dated action, and a contingency that is already designed rather than improvised. Cannot be accepted | Action now; escalation to the project lead on identification |
  ZH: | **4** | 極可能——依現有證據預期會發生 | 嚴重——失去一項「必須」帶能力、一項驗收標準或提交本身 | **Critical（12–16）**：立即行動，附具名擁有人及有日期的行動，以及一項已設計好而非臨時拼湊的應變方案。不可接受 | 立即行動；識別時即上呈項目負責人 |
L1176 DIFF
  EN: | **3** | Likely — more than an even chance | Major — a should-band capability or a phase gate is lost, or several days of work are lost | **High (8–9)**: mitigate within the current phase, with a contingency named in the register | Action within the phase; contingency documented by the end of the phase |
  ZH: | **3** | 可能——機率高於一半 | 重大——失去一項「應該」帶能力或一道階段閘門，或損失數日工作 | **High（8–9）**：於當前階段內緩解，並在登記冊中指明應變方案 | 於階段內行動；應變方案於階段結束前記錄 |
L1177 DIFF
  EN: | **2** | Possible — could occur | Moderate — a could-band capability is lost, or a day or two is lost | **Medium (4–6)**: monitor, with a stated trigger that would raise the score | Reviewed weekly; contingency described |
  ZH: | **2** | 有可能——可能發生 | 中等——失去一項「可以」帶能力，或損失一至兩日 | **Medium（4–6）**：監控，並聲明會提高分數的觸發條件 | 每週檢視；應變方案已描述 |
L1178 DIFF
  EN: | **1** | Unlikely — no evidence it will occur | Minor — cosmetic or documentary, or absorbed without effect on scope | **Low (1–3)**: accept and monitor | Accepted; no action required |
  ZH: | **1** | 不太可能——沒有證據顯示會發生 | 輕微——外觀或文件性質，或可被吸收而不影響範圍 | **Low（1–3）**：接受並監控 | 已接受；無需行動 |
L1179 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L1180 DIFF
  EN: **4. Response types.** Each risk carries one of five responses, recorded explicitly: **avoid** (change the design so that the risk cannot occur), **mitigate** (reduce likelihood or impact), **accept** (with the consequence stated rather than implied), **monitor** (no action yet, but a stated trigger), or **exploit** (turn the uncertainty into a deliberate, timeboxed experiment, which is how several of this project's uncertainties are converted into legitimate experiment results instead of lost weeks). Transfer through insurance is not available to a student team and is not claimed.
  ZH: **4. 應對類型。** 每項風險帶有五種應對之一，並明確記錄：**避免**（改變設計使該風險不可能發生）、**緩解**（降低可能性或影響）、**接受**（連同已聲明而非暗示的後果）、**監控**（暫不行動，但聲明觸發條件），或**利用**（把該不確定性轉化為刻意且限時的實驗，本項目若干不確定性正是如此轉化為正當的實驗結果，而非白白損失的週數）。透過保險轉移對學生小組而言並不可行，亦不予聲稱。

L1223 DIFF
  EN: **Mapping to the design baseline's own risk table.** The baseline's risks were written as design risks; the register above carries them all and adds project-level risks. The mapping is deliberate and is recorded so that nothing from the baseline is silently dropped: baseline R-01 → PR-06; R-02 → PR-04; R-03 → PR-14; R-04 → PR-01; R-05 → PR-08; R-06 → PR-09; R-07 → PR-10; R-08 → PR-11; R-09 → PR-12; R-10 → PR-13. The baseline's assumption table maps to the register's external and resource rows (PR-06, PR-14, PR-15, PR-16, PR-21, PR-22), and its limitation tables — simulator limits, and the physical/logical approximations including the single-host availability limitation — are the substance of PR-02 and PR-03.
  ZH: **與設計基準自身風險表的對應。** 基準的風險是作為設計風險撰寫的；上述登記冊承載全部並加入項目層級風險。該對應是刻意的並予記錄，使基準中沒有任何項目被悄悄丟棄：基準 R-01 → PR-06；R-02 → PR-04；R-03 → PR-14；R-04 → PR-01；R-05 → PR-08；R-06 → PR-09；R-07 → PR-10；R-08 → PR-11；R-09 → PR-12；R-10 → PR-13。基準的假設表對應至登記冊的外部及資源列（PR-06、PR-14、PR-15、PR-16、PR-21、PR-22），而其限制表——模擬器限制，以及包括單主機可用性限制在內的實體／邏輯近似——即為 PR-02 及 PR-03 的實質內容。

L1225 DIFF

  ZH: **按嚴重性的登記冊摘要。** Critical 及 High 列佔 24 項中的 14 項。在一個有兩條技術線、一項模擬限制及一條執行環境無法事先驗證的概念驗證線的十二週項目中，這是預期之中的，而這正是第 12.9 節的削減順序在需要之前即已固定的原因。

  EN: **1.   ZH: **1. AI 線（PR-01）——唯一其核心元件無法事先驗證的線。** 設計基準明確指出，代理執行環境及其 Skills 模型是本項目最無法驗證的元素：在撰寫時，該執行環境的安裝方法、skill 檔案格式、預設埠、授權，甚至一個穩定版本，均無法自撰寫環境確認，而基準聲明：關於它的任何版本、URL、授權或能力聲明，均不得在沒有本實驗室中運行實例的截圖及實際所用文件之引用的情況下出現於報告。埠與協定矩陣中對應的服務埠標示為假設，且必須依運行實例更正。
L1230 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L1231 DIFF
  EN: The risk is not that the work is hard; it is that the work has a **binary dependency on a third-party runtime that the group does not control and cannot fully test in advance**, and that the brief explicitly names it as one of the five technology lines. Three mitigations make this manageable rather than paralysing. First, the design puts the **tool surface, not the runtime, at the centre**: four declared read-only tools with declared input schemas, which any of three implementations can drive. Second, the spike's output is a **decision, not a working system**, and the decision deadline is end of week 6, so a failure costs three days rather than three weeks. Third, the fallbacks are already designed — a generic agent loop over the same four tools, or the deterministic collectors and correlation rules with a scripted policy standing in for the agent. The third fallback is the important one: it means that even in the worst case the project still delivers a **verifiable, useful, documented capability**, and the AI chapter becomes an honest evaluation of an agentic design against a runtime that could not be evaluated. The baseline's own guidance applies: documenting a failure is a stronger academic result than hiding one.
  ZH: 風險不在於這項工作困難；而在於這項工作對一個**小組無法控制、且無法事先充分測試的第三方執行環境有二元依賴**，且簡介明確將其列為五條技術線之一。三項緩解措施使之可控而非令人癱瘓。第一，設計把**工具介面、而非執行環境，放在中心**：四個已聲明的唯讀工具及已聲明的輸入 schema，三種實作中任一均可驅動。第二，尖峰測試的產出是一項**決定，而非可運作的系統**，且決策期限為第 6 週結束，因此失敗的代價是三天而非三週。第三，備援方案已經設計好——在同一組四個工具之上的通用代理迴路，或以腳本化政策代替代理的確定性收集器及關聯規則。第三項備援方案是最重要的一項：它意味著即使在最壞情況下，本項目仍交付**一項可驗證、有用且有記錄的能力**，而 AI 章節則成為針對一個無法評估之執行環境，對某項代理式設計所作的誠實評估。基準自身的指引適用：記錄一項失敗是比隱藏它更強的學術成果。
L1232 SAME
  EN:

L1233 DIFF
  EN: **2. The all-virtual, no-physical-hardware constraint (PR-02) — an honest-evidence risk rather than a build risk.** Nothing in this project may be ordered, assumed present, or required for any verification step. That removes an entire class of practical problems, but it creates a subtler one: the temptation to describe emulated behaviour in the voice of measured behaviour, and the corresponding risk that an assessor dismisses the whole design as "not real". The project answers this by making the boundary explicit and by putting it in every chapter rather than in one disclaimer. The VM estate is genuinely configured and running; the Cisco estate is simulated; segmentation for real hosts is emulated by separate hypervisor networks rather than by 802.1Q tags; and physical hardware, real line-rate forwarding and real Internet connectivity are simply absent. Each of those four statements is a design decision with a reason, and each is labelled wherever a result touches it.
  ZH: **2. 全虛擬、無實體硬體的限制（PR-02）——是誠實證據的風險，而非建構風險。** 本項目中沒有任何東西需要訂購、假定存在，或被任何驗證步驟所需要。這消除了整整一類實務問題，卻也產生一個更微妙的問題：以量測行為的語氣描述模擬行為的誘惑，以及評核者把整個設計斥為「不真實」的相應風險。本項目使該邊界明確，並將其放入每一章而非一則免責聲明，以此回應。VM 環境是真實配置並運行；Cisco 環境是模擬；真實主機的分段由獨立的虛擬化平台網路而非 802.1Q 標籤模擬；而實體硬體、真實線速轉發及真實互聯網連線則完全不存在。這四項陳述各自都是一項附理由的設計決定，且每一項在其結果所涉及之處均予標註。

  EN:

L1235 DIFF
  EN: The risk is scored Critical on impact rather than likelihood because a failure here is not a lost test — it is a lost *claim*, and a claim that collapses takes the surrounding chapter with it. The mitigation is to keep claims proportionate to evidence: availability is discussed as *service* redundancy that is genuinely demonstrated (VIP failover, a second domain controller, a second proxy) and separately as *network-path* redundancy that is only simulated, and no availability figure is claimed for the single hypervisor host at all. The report states that the host is a single point of failure for the entire estate, that source artefacts are protected by version control and off-host mirrors, and that this protection applies to the **project** and not to **service availability**. Saying that plainly is what keeps the rest of the technical claims credible.
  ZH: 該風險在影響而非可能性上被評為 Critical，因為此處的失敗不是失去一項測試——而是失去一項*主張*，而一項崩塌的主張會連同其周邊章節一起崩塌。緩解方式是使主張與證據成比例：可用性作為真實展示的*服務*冗餘（VIP 故障轉移、第二部網域控制站、第二個代理）討論，並另行作為僅屬模擬的*網路路徑*冗餘討論，且完全不就單一虛擬化平台主機聲稱任何可用性數字。報告聲明該主機是整個環境的單點故障、來源產物受版本控制及離主機鏡像保護，而該保護適用於**項目**而非**服務可用性**。明確如此說明正是使其餘技術主張保持可信的原因。

  EN:

L1237 DIFF
  EN: **3. Packet Tracer fidelity (PR-03) — a ceiling on what can be claimed, not a defect in the work.** The simulator models CLI syntax and protocol logic; it does not model application-specific silicon, line-rate forwarding, real hardware, real clock behaviour or real-world convergence times. Its ASA device is a reduced legacy model: clustering, active/standby failover pairs with state replication, the newer threat-detection engines and much of the drop-level detail are absent or incomplete. Its BGP support covers neighbour establishment, network statements, prefix lists, route maps and simple path attributes but not route reflectors, confederations or large-scale peering. It has no SSH server reachable from external tooling and no API at all, which is precisely why the automation line is documented as a two-track approach and why the report must state that **no playbook ever logs in to a Packet Tracer device**.
  ZH: **3. Packet Tracer 保真度（PR-03）——是可主張內容的上限，而非工作上的缺陷。** 模擬器塑造 CLI 語法及協定邏輯；它不塑造應用專用矽晶、線速轉發、真實硬體、真實時鐘行為或真實世界的收斂時間。其 ASA 裝置是精簡的舊式模型：叢集、具狀態複寫的主動／待命故障轉移對、較新的威脅偵測引擎及大部分 drop 級細節均缺席或不完整。其 BGP 支持涵蓋相鄰建立、network 陳述、前綴清單、route map 及簡單路徑屬性，但不含 route reflector、confederation 或大規模對等互連。它沒有可供外部工具連接的 SSH 伺服器，也完全沒有 API，這正是自動化線被記錄為雙軌方針，以及報告必須聲明**沒有任何 playbook 會登入 Packet Tracer 裝置**的原因。
L1238 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L1239 DIFF
  EN: Two disciplines convert this ceiling into a managed constraint. The first is **capability verification before testing**: the exact device model and image string are read from `show version` on the first build day and any divergence from the design corrects the design, so the project never tests against a capability it assumed. The second is **the right kind of measurement**: because simulator timings are artefacts, convergence is demonstrated by the *order of topology-change events* — which port becomes root, which becomes alternate, which becomes forwarding — rather than by a millisecond figure presented as a real-world number. The risk that remains is schedule, not truth: a capability the group expected and the simulator lacks can consume a day. That is why every experiment is timeboxed to half a day and a negative result is recorded as an experiment result, because a documented limitation is worth more than an unexplained absence.
  ZH: 兩項紀律把這個上限轉化為受管理的限制。第一是**測試前的功能驗證**：確切的裝置型號及映像字串在建構第一日自 `show version` 讀取，而任何與設計的差異均更正設計，使本項目永不針對它所假設的功能進行測試。第二是**正確的量測類型**：因為模擬器時間屬產物，收斂以*拓撲變更事件的順序*展示——哪個埠成為根、哪個成為替代、哪個成為 forwarding——而非以被當作真實世界數字的毫秒數字展示。仍然存在的風險是進度，而非真實性：一項小組預期而模擬器缺乏的功能可以耗掉一天。這就是為什麼每項實驗均限時半日，且負面結果記錄為實驗結果，因為有記錄的限制比無法解釋的缺席更有價值。

L1241 DIFF
  EN: **4. The twelve-week schedule (PR-04) — a breadth problem, not an effort problem.** The design deliberately spans five technology lines, 19 virtual machines, 16 simulated network devices, 153 test-case identifiers and a documentation set with six required categories. Attempting full depth on all of that in twelve weeks is the baseline's own identified High/High risk, and the group's judgment is that the schedule is achievable **only because the scope was prioritised before the work started rather than during it**. The four-band order — must, should, could, won't — is therefore a schedule control, not a wish list, and the cut order in section 12.9 is applied from the bottom of the should band upwards.
  ZH: **4. 十二週進度表（PR-04）——是廣度問題，而非工作量問題。** 設計刻意橫跨五條技術線、19 部虛擬機、16 部模擬網路裝置、153 個測試案例識別碼，以及一個含六個必要類別的文件集。試圖在十二週內對這一切做到完整深度，正是基準自身所識別的 High／High 風險，而小組的判斷是：該進度表**只有在範圍於工作開始之前而非期間被排定優先次序的情況下**才可達成。因此四個帶的順序——必須、應該、可以、不會做——是一項進度控制，而非願望清單，且第 12.9 節的削減順序自「應該」帶底部向上套用。
L1242 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L1243 DIFF
  EN: Three structural choices reduce the risk further. Phases are ordered along the service dependency chain so that at most one phase is ever blocked, which converts parallel work from a coordination hazard into a genuine schedule benefit. The interface contract freezes at the end of week 6 and no new technology is added after week 9, which prevents the most common late-project failure: an appealing addition in week 8 that invalidates two weeks of testing. And the must band is defined narrowly enough to be defensible — core identity roles, OSPF, RSTP and firewall policy, the reverse proxy with first-hop redundancy and firewalling, three scripts and three playbooks, two demonstrated AI capabilities, and the complete documentation and evidence set — so that a project that delivers the must band and honestly declares the rest is a **complete** project rather than a truncated one.
  ZH: 三項結構性選擇進一步降低風險。各階段沿服務相依鏈排序，使任何時候最多只有一個階段被阻塞，這把並行工作從協調風險轉化為真正的進度優勢。介面契約於第 6 週結束時凍結，且第 9 週之後不加入任何新技術，這防止了最常見的項目後期失敗：第 8 週一項吸引人的新增，使兩週的測試失效。而「必須」帶的定義足夠狹窄而站得住腳——核心身分角色、OSPF、RSTP 及防火牆政策、附第一跳冗餘及防火牆的反向代理、三個腳本及三個 playbook、兩項已展示的 AI 能力，以及完整的文件及證據集——使一個交付「必須」帶並誠實聲明其餘部分的項目成為**完整**的項目，而非被截斷的項目。
L1244 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L1245 DIFF
  EN: **5. Four-person coordination (PR-05) — the risk that no individual test can detect.** Four people working on one integrated system fail in ways that no single test reveals: two members implement the same thing differently, a dependency is assumed by both and owned by neither, a naming divergence propagates into configuration and evidence, and — the failure that matters most to assessment — individual contribution becomes impossible to demonstrate. Each of these is addressed by a named mechanism rather than by goodwill: a frozen contract of values so that naming cannot diverge; a RACI matrix with exactly one accountable member per row so that no dependency is unowned; a pairing and shadow arrangement in which each partner executes the other's artefact at least once, so that cross-cover is demonstrated rather than asserted; and a weekly integration session with a whole-estate verification run, so that integration failure is detected in the week that caused it. The individual-contribution problem is answered by five overlapping evidence sources — named personal administrative accounts, accountable ownership per deliverable, disjoint section authorship, evidence ownership with performer initials, and commit history — described in section 16.4, so that no single mechanism has to carry the whole argument. The residual risk is interpersonal rather than technical, and the response is procedural: the weekly stand-up exists specifically to surface blockers early, and the standard's own expectation that a professional seeks help from peers when lacking experience in an area is treated as a working practice rather than as an admission.
  ZH: **5. 四人協調（PR-05）——沒有個別測試能偵測的風險。** 四個人共同處理一套整合系統，會以沒有任何單一測試能揭示的方式失敗：兩名成員以不同方式實作同一件事、一項相依被雙方假設卻無人擁有、命名分歧傳播至配置及證據，以及——對評核最重要的失敗——個人貢獻變得無法展示。這些各以具名機制而非善意來處理：一份凍結的數值契約使命名無法分歧；一個每列恰好一名當責成員的 RACI 矩陣使沒有相依無人擁有；一項配對及影子安排使每個夥伴至少執行另一方的產物一次，從而使交叉替補被展示而非僅被斷言；以及每週一次附整個環境驗證執行的整合會議，使整合失敗在其成因所屬的那一週被偵測。個人貢獻問題由五項互相重疊的證據來源作答——具名的個人管理帳戶、每項交付物的當責擁有權、互不重疊的章節撰寫權、附執行者姓名縮寫的證據擁有權，以及提交歷史——於第 16.4 節描述，使沒有任何單一機制必須承擔整個論證。餘下風險屬人際而非技術性質，而應對屬程序性質：每週站立會議正是為了及早浮現阻塞事項而存在，而標準自身期望專業人士在某領域缺乏經驗時向同儕求助，這被視為一種工作實務而非一種招認。

L1247 DIFF
  EN: **Residual and accepted risks.** Four risks are accepted rather than mitigated, and are recorded as accepted so that acceptance is a decision rather than an oversight: the single-hypervisor-host availability limitation, which no amount of process can remove; the absence of any UPS, power distribution or physical console, which makes power and physical-layer scenarios unimplementable; the absence of real public addressing and real certificate authority validation, which is a deliberate consequence of using documentation address ranges and reserved name suffixes; and the absence of a real Internet path, which means the AI line's model access — if an external API is used — depends on something the lab does not control, which is exactly why the deterministic fallback exists.
  ZH: **餘下及已接受的風險。** 有四項風險被接受而非緩解，並記錄為已接受，使接受成為一項決定而非疏忽：單一虛擬化平台主機的可用性限制，任何程序都無法移除；沒有任何 UPS、配電或實體主控台，使電力及實體層情境無法實作；沒有真實公眾定址及真實憑證授權單位驗證，這是使用文件專用位址範圍及保留名稱後綴的刻意結果；以及沒有真實互聯網路徑，這意味著 AI 線的模型存取——若使用外部 API——取決於實驗室無法控制的某樣東西，這正是確定性備援方案存在的原因。
L1248 SAME
  EN:

L1249 DIFF
  EN: ### 15.4 Issues Log
  ZH: ### 15.4 問題日誌
L1250 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L1251 DIFF
  EN: A **risk** is something that might happen; an **issue** is something that has happened. The distinction governs the response: a risk is reviewed, scored and given a contingency that may never be used, whereas an issue is assigned, worked and closed, and it consumes real time immediately. A risk that occurs becomes an issue and is closed in this log; the risk row is then marked as realised and retained, because the fact that it happened is itself information. Confusing the two produces the classic failure of a register full of past events and a project with no forward view; this project keeps them separate and reviews both weekly.
  ZH: **風險**是可能發生的事情；**問題**是已經發生的事情。這一區別決定應對方式：風險受檢視、評分並給予可能永不使用的應變方案，而問題則被指派、處理並結案，且立即耗用真實時間。已發生的風險成為問題並在本日誌中結案；該風險列隨後標示為已實現並保留，因為它曾發生這一事實本身就是資訊。把二者混淆會產生典型的失敗：一本充滿過去事件的登記冊，以及一個沒有前瞻視野的項目；本項目將二者分開並每週檢視兩者。

  EN: 
  ZH:

L1273 DIFF
  EN: **Worked illustrative issue.** `IS-03 — VPN client connect fails with a certificate error.` Raised as a test failure during the IKEv2 acceptance test: the client's connection attempt returned a certificate-validation error rather than a logon prompt. Impact: the IKEv2 remote-access test could not run, and two dependent test cases were blocked; approximately half a day lost. Not blocking overall build, because the OpenVPN path was independent. Owner: Member A. Action agreed: verify the certificate template's subject alternative names against the certificate-subject plan, confirm that the client had auto-enrolled the machine template, and re-run the connection test after enrolment. Resolution: the client had not yet received the auto-enrolment policy because it was in a group that the policy did not target; the test client was moved into the correct organisational unit and the policy was confirmed with a group-policy result report before the test was re-run. Evidence: the re-executed test record, the group-policy result report screenshot, and the successful connection transcript. Status: closed. Linked risk: none — which is itself recorded, because the correct response to an issue with no matching risk is to ask why the risk review did not anticipate it.
  ZH: **實例說明問題。** `IS-03 — VPN client connect fails with a certificate error.` 於 IKEv2 驗收測試期間作為一項測試失敗提出：用戶端的連接嘗試回傳憑證驗證錯誤，而非登入提示。影響：IKEv2 遠端存取測試無法執行，且兩項相依測試案例被阻塞；損失約半日。整體建構不受阻塞，因為 OpenVPN 路徑是獨立的。擁有人：Member A。已協定的行動：依憑證主體計劃核對憑證範本的主體別名、確認用戶端已自動註冊機器範本，並在註冊後重跑連接測試。解決：用戶端因位於該政策未針對的群組中，而尚未收到自動註冊政策；測試用戶端已移入正確的組織單位，並在重跑測試之前以群組政策結果報告確認該政策。證據：重新執行的測試記錄、群組政策結果報告截圖，以及成功的連接逐字稿。狀態：已結案。連結風險：無——此事本身亦予記錄，因為對一項沒有對應風險的問題，正確的回應是追問風險檢視為何沒有預見它。

L1304 DIFF
  EN: The group has four members and delivers five technology lines plus the documentation and project-management workstreams. Roles are therefore **functional**: a role is a responsibility that has to be discharged, and the technology lines are distributed so that each role carries a comparable load. Membership is identified as Member A to Member D so that the role descriptions remain stable if names change.
  ZH: 小組有四名成員，交付五條技術線，加上文件及項目管理工作流。因此角色是**功能性**的：一個角色是一項必須履行的責任，而技術線的分派使每個角色承擔相稱的負荷。成員以 Member A 至 Member D 標示，使角色描述在姓名變更時仍保持穩定。

L1312 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L1313 DIFF
  EN: **Two notes on the structure.** First, **project lead and integration owner are one role, held by Member A**, because integration failure is the project's principal coordination risk and the person who chairs the review should also be accountable for integration working. Second, **the documentation integration role is held by Member D rather than by a fifth person**, which means Member D carries the heaviest administrative load; the compensating arrangement is that every member owns their own evidence and section authorship, and Member D integrates rather than writes the whole set. That split is stated explicitly so that the load is visible rather than hidden inside the word "documentation".
  ZH: **關於此結構的兩點說明。** 第一，**項目負責人與整合擁有人是同一角色，由 Member A 擔任**，因為整合失敗是本項目主要的協調風險，而主持該審查的人也應對整合成功當責。第二，**文件整合角色由 Member D 而非第五個人擔任**，這意味著 Member D 承擔最重的行政負荷；補償安排是每名成員擁有自己的證據及章節撰寫權，而 Member D 負責整合而非撰寫整套文件。該分工予以明確聲明，使該負荷可見，而非隱藏於「文件」一詞之內。
L1314 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L1315 DIFF
  EN: **Governance rules the structure depends on.** One member is accountable for every deliverable and one other member reviews it. The interface contract of values — hostnames, addresses, VLAN identifiers, AS numbers, account names, policy names and the banned-string list — may not be changed by any member acting alone: a change requires a decision-log entry, three-of-four agreement, supervisor awareness and a re-issued revision, because the failure mode this project must avoid is four documents that contradict one another.
  ZH: **此結構所依賴的管治規則。** 每項交付物由一名成員當責，並由另一名成員審查。數值介面契約——主機名稱、位址、VLAN 識別碼、AS 號碼、帳戶名稱、政策名稱及禁用字串清單——不得由任何成員單獨變更：一項變更需要決策日誌記錄、四人中三人的同意、導師知情，以及重新簽發的修訂版本，因為本項目必須避免的失敗模式，就是四份互相矛盾的報告。

L1335 DIFF
  EN: **Channel discipline.** A decision is only valid when it is in the repository. L1348 DIFF
  EN: **A note about actors.** The project separates the **real** approval chain from the **in-scenario** one. The supervisor approves scope, receives gate reviews and signs off the delivered work. The IT manager role belongs to the client scenario: it supplies the design's business driver and provides a realistic user-acceptance conversation. The project does not claim the involvement of any real external organisation, and no real user data is handled at any point.
  ZH: **關於行為者的一點說明。** 本項目把**真實**批准鏈與**情境中**批准鏈分開。導師批准範圍、接收閘門審查並簽核所交付的工作。IT 經理角色屬於客戶情境：它提供設計的商業驅動力，並提供一場真實的使用者驗收對話。本項目不聲稱涉及任何真實外部組織，且在任何時刻均不處理任何真實使用者資料。

  EN: ### 16.4 Individual Contribution and Assessment
  ZH: ### 16.4 個人貢獻與評核
L1351 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L1352 DIFF
  EN: Individual contribution normally has to be evidenced, and a four-person project on one integrated system makes that genuinely difficult: the system succeeds or fails as a whole, so contribution cannot be read off the outcome. This project answers with five **overlapping** evidence sources, so that no single source has to carry the argument and no member's mark depends on a colleague's memory.
  ZH: 個人貢獻通常必須有證據支持，而一個四人共同處理一套整合系統的項目使這確實困難：該系統整體地成功或失敗，因此貢獻無法從結果中讀出。本項目以五項**互相重疊**的證據來源作答，使沒有任何單一來源必須承擔論證，也沒有任何成員的評分取決於同事的記憶。

L1376 DIFF
  EN: Its purpose is to make the distribution of work visible early enough to correct it: if the matrix shows one member with half the owned work packages of the others at week 8, the response is a re-balance recorded in the minutes, not an explanation written in week 12. In the report, the matrix is presented with its sources cited, and each member's technical chapter is presented under their own accountability. The honest position is stated once and not repeated defensively: a four-person integrated project is assessed both as a system and as four contributions, and this project's contribution record is designed to support the second assessment without weakening the first.
  ZH: 其目的是使工作分配及早可見以便糾正：若該矩陣在第 8 週顯示某名成員所擁有的工作包是其他人的一半，應對方式是在會議紀錄中記錄一次重新平衡，而非在第 12 週寫一份解釋。在報告中，該矩陣連同其引用來源一同呈現，而每名成員的技術章節在其自身的當責之下呈現。誠實的立場聲明一次且不防禦性地重複：四人的整合項目既作為一套系統、也作為四份貢獻受評核，而本項目的貢獻記錄之設計，是在不削弱第一種評核的情況下支持第二種評核。
