  EN: ### 16.5 Health, Safety, Legal and Ethical Considerations
  ZH: ### 16.5 健康、安全、法律及倫理考量
L1379 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L1380 DIFF
  EN: This project is a virtualisation and simulation exercise performed on student-owned computers, so the safety discussion is short and concrete rather than ornamental. The relevant obligations appear in the standard's own work-organisation section, which places health and safety duties, safe working practice, the care of tools and equipment and the integrity of user equipment and information among the first requirements an IT network systems administrator must meet.
  ZH: 本項目是在學生自有的電腦上進行的虛擬化及模擬演習，因此安全討論簡短而具體，而非裝飾性的。相關義務出現於標準自身的工作組織章節，該章節把健康及安全職責、安全工作實務、工具及設備的愛護，以及使用者設備及資訊的完整性，列為 IT 網路系統管理員必須滿足的首批要求。
L1381 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L1382 DIFF
  EN: **Health and safety in a virtual context.** The hazards at issue are not electrical or mechanical, because no physical network hardware, no cabling and no server-room work is involved. The real hazards are of the working-environment kind. Workstation posture and ergonomics are observed during prolonged configuration sessions, with breaks taken between long tasks. Display and lighting conditions are set to reduce eye strain during extended reading of terminal output and log files. No mains or internal hardware work is performed on any machine. Where a member does handle components — for example adding memory to a hypervisor host — the standard's own guidance applies: the machine is powered down and unplugged and electrostatic discharge precautions are used, because electrostatic damage is the one genuine physical hazard a project of this shape can encounter. Electrical safety follows the manufacturer's instructions and no cable, socket or power supply is modified. Data safety is treated as a safety matter too: the laboratory is isolated from the campus and home networks by design, bridging is forbidden, and no experiment is directed at any system the group does not own.
  ZH: **虛擬情境中的健康與安全。** 所涉危害並非電氣或機械性質，因為不涉及任何實體網路硬體、佈線或機房工作。真正的危害屬工作環境性質。在長時間配置時段中注意工作站姿勢及人體工學，並在長任務之間休息。在長時間閱讀終端輸出及日誌檔時，設定顯示及照明條件以減少眼睛疲勞。不對任何機器進行市電或內部硬體作業。若成員確實需處理元件——例如為虛擬化平台主機加裝記憶體——則適用標準自身的指引：機器關機並拔除插頭，且使用靜電放電防護措施，因為靜電損害是這種形態的項目所能遇到的一項真正實體危害。電氣安全依製造商指示，且不修改任何線纜、插座或電源供應器。資料安全亦被視為安全事項：實驗室在設計上與校園及家用網路隔離、禁止橋接，且不針對小組並不擁有的任何系統進行任何實驗。
L1383 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L1384 DIFF
  EN: **Software licensing.** The project is explicit: **only properly licensed software, free-of-charge software, or software that is free for personal use is installed**, and no commercial licence is purchased or claimed. Concretely: the hypervisor is used under its free-for-personal-use entitlement, which is re-checked at build time, with the named alternative being free open-source virtualisation rather than a purchase; server operating systems are used under a time-limited evaluation licence or the host institution's volume media entitlement; the Linux estate uses distribution packages and freely licensed components throughout; the network estate is configured inside a simulator provided through the group's academy account and no third-party or mirrored installer is used; and the open-source components — reverse proxy, first-hop redundancy, packet filtering, tunnel software, certificate tooling, monitoring, configuration management and version control — are used under their respective licences. Because one component in the innovation line had genuinely uncertain licensing provenance at design time, the project rule is strict: no version, capability or licence statement about it may appear in any deliverable without a screenshot of the running instance and a citation to the documentation actually used. Where an entitlement could not be verified, the design names a free fallback rather than assuming a right to install.
  ZH: **軟件授權。** 本項目明確表示：**只安裝經適當授權的軟件、免費軟件，或個人使用免費的軟件**，且不購買亦不聲稱任何商業授權。具體而言：虛擬化平台依其個人使用免費權益使用，並在建構時重新檢查，指名的替代方案是免費開源虛擬化而非購買；伺服器作業系統依限時評估授權或主辦機構的大量授權媒體權益使用；Linux 環境全程使用發行版套件及自由授權元件；網路環境在小組的學院帳戶所提供的模擬器內部配置，且不使用任何第三方或鏡像安裝程式；而開源元件——反向代理、第一跳冗餘、封包過濾、隧道軟件、憑證工具、監控、配置管理及版本控制——依其各自的授權使用。由於創新線中有一項元件在設計時的授權來源確實不確定，本項目的規則嚴格：未經運行實例的截圖及對實際所用文件的引用，任何交付物中均不得出現關於它的版本、能力或授權聲明。若某項權益無法核實，設計即指明一個免費備援方案，而非假定有安裝的權利。

  EN:

L1386 DIFF
  EN: **Data protection.** The laboratory uses **fictional data only**. There is no real customer data, no real personal data, no production database and no live system anywhere in the project. Names, account identifiers, document content and application data are generated by the group for testing; the scenario organisation is fictional; and no record retrieved from any real system is used. Addresses come from the documentation address ranges and published service names use a reserved name suffix, so no real organisation's name or address space appears. This is a design constraint rather than an aspiration, and it is verifiable: a repository search for real domain names, real public addresses and real personal data is part of the pre-release sweep.
  ZH: **資料保護。** 實驗室**僅使用虛構資料**。本項目中任何地方都沒有真實客戶資料、沒有真實個人資料、沒有生產資料庫，也沒有實際運行的系統。姓名、帳戶識別碼、文件內容及應用程式資料均由小組為測試而產生；情境組織是虛構的；且不使用任何自真實系統取回的記錄。位址取自文件專用位址範圍，而已發佈的服務名稱使用保留名稱後綴，因此沒有任何真實組織的名稱或位址空間出現。這是設計限制而非願景，且它可被驗證：在儲存庫中搜尋真實網域名稱、真實公眾位址及真實個人資料，是發行前清查的一部分。
L1387 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L1388 DIFF
  EN: **Academic integrity.** Four rules apply without exception. First, **all technical results are traceable to a command transcript or a screenshot**; no result is reported that cannot be reproduced from its recorded procedure. Second, **no generated content is presented as measured data**: where an assistance tool has been used to draft or restructure text, that use is disclosed, and generated text is never the source of a technical figure — figures come from the estate. Third, **all sources are genuinely consulted and cited**, with no address written from memory and no reference presented as read when it was only quoted elsewhere. Fourth, **failures are reported**: an incorrect output from the innovation line, a test that did not pass, a version that differs from the register and a capability that was cut are all recorded, because presenting a limitation honestly is a stronger academic result than reporting a false pass.
  ZH: **學術誠信。** 四項規則無例外地適用。第一，**所有技術結果均可追溯至命令逐字稿或截圖**；不報告任何無法自其已記錄程序重現的結果。第二，**不將生成的內容當作量測資料呈現**：若曾使用輔助工具草擬或重組文字，該使用予以披露，而生成的文字絕不是技術數字的來源——數字來自環境。第三，**所有來源均確實查閱並引用**，沒有憑記憶寫出的位址，也沒有將僅從他處轉引的參考文獻呈現為已閱讀。第四，**失敗予以報告**：來自創新線的錯誤輸出、一項未通過的測試、一個與登記冊不同的版本，以及一項被削減的能力，全部予以記錄，因為誠實呈現一項限制是比報告虛假通過更強的學術成果。
L1389 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L1390 DIFF
  EN: **Professional ethics.** Deliberate-misuse experimentation is conducted ethically: the failed-login burst, the configuration-drift test, the rogue address-assignment test and the management-port scan are all performed against the group's own laboratory segments, are documented in advance, and are sourced from the deliberately isolated segment that the design itself keeps unprivileged. No test is directed at any system the group does not own, and no service is probed beyond the laboratory boundary. Least privilege is applied to people as well as services: administrative accounts are personal, two of the four members hold the highest tier of access because they administer the identity layer, and the reason and its limits are documented rather than glossed over. Break-glass access is sealed and unused during normal operation, with any use logged in the report. The project also states plainly what it is **not**: the design is *aligned with* selected security controls; it is not certified against them, it makes no compliance claim, and no penetration test is claimed. That is a professional-ethics statement before it is a legal one — the temptation to imply more than was delivered is the specific failure this project is structured to avoid.
  ZH: **專業倫理。** 刻意誤用實驗以合乎倫理的方式進行：登入失敗串、配置漂移測試、流氓位址指派測試及管理埠掃描，全部針對小組自身的實驗室網段執行、事先記錄，並源自設計本身即保持無特權的刻意隔離網段。不針對小組並不擁有的任何系統進行任何測試，也不在實驗室邊界之外探測任何服務。最小權限既適用於人亦適用於服務：管理帳戶是個人性質的，四名成員中有兩人持有最高存取層級，因為他們管理身分層，而其理由及限制予以記錄而非含糊帶過。緊急破窗存取已密封且在正常運作期間不使用，任何使用均記錄於報告中。本項目亦明確聲明它**不是**什麼：本設計*與*所選安全控制項目*對齊*；它並未就該等項目獲得認證、不作任何合規聲明，亦不聲稱進行過任何滲透測試。這首先是專業倫理的聲明，其次才是法律聲明——暗示超出實際交付的內容這種誘惑，正是本項目在結構上要避免的特定失敗。

L1415 DIFF
  EN: **Stated assumptions behind the estimate.** The hypervisor host is already owned and is not a project purchase. Machines run only when work is being done and the estate is powered down outside build and test windows, so the electricity figure reflects a disciplined rather than a continuous operating pattern. No software licence, support contract, subscription or hardware purchase is planned. No cost is attributed to member time, because twelve weeks of academic effort cannot be priced meaningfully without inventing a rate. The estimate excludes the sunk cost of members' existing laptops and any cost of the institution's network and facilities, both of which the project uses without charge. The two figures that would change the estimate materially are a decision to buy additional memory to reach the recommended 64 GB, and the failure of a member's machine; both are recorded in the assumptions log and the risk register rather than left implicit.
  ZH: **估算背後的已聲明假設。** 虛擬化平台主機已擁有，並非本項目的採購。機器只在有工作進行時運行，且環境在建構及測試窗口之外關機，因此電力數字反映的是有紀律而非持續的運作模式。不計劃任何軟件授權、支援合約、訂閱或硬體採購。不為成員時間計入任何成本，因為十二週的學術投入若無虛構一個費率便無法有意義地定價。該估算不計成員現有筆記型電腦的沉沒成本，以及機構網路及設施的任何成本，兩者本項目均免費使用。會實質改變該估算的兩個數字，是決定購買額外記憶體以達到建議的 64 GB，以及成員機器的故障；兩者均記錄於假設日誌及風險登記冊，而非任其隱含。

L1472 DIFF
  EN: 2. **The screen-capture requirement comes from the project brief, not from the standard.** The strings `screen`, `capture`, `screenshot` and `evidence` occur zero times on the standard's page.   ZH: 2. **螢幕擷取要求來自項目簡介，而非該標準。** `screen`、`capture`、`screenshot` 及 `evidence` 等字串在該標準頁面出現零次。證據集之所以存在，是因為簡介要求它，而本項目不把該要求歸屬於該標準。
L1473 DIFF
  EN: 3. **Several technologies in this design are not named by the standard at all**, including file-server quota and screening, network block storage, certificate-based remote-access service on the Microsoft platform, host-based packet filtering, the tunnel implementation used for the small-office peer, rapid spanning-tree, the firewall appliance platform and the simulator itself. Each is justified from the project brief and from generic requirement lines, and each bridge is presented as an inference from a quoted line rather than as a quotation. Two of the bridges are strong — the standard names VRRP, which the Linux first-hop redundancy tool implements, and it names the switching layers that the spanning-tree design satisfies — and one is weak: the tunnel used for the site-to-office peer is covered only by the `etc.` at the end of the standard's VPN line, and the report says so rather than implying stronger coverage.
  ZH: 3. **本設計中有若干技術根本未被該標準指名的技術**，包括檔案伺服器配額及屏蔽、網路區塊儲存、Microsoft 平台上以憑證為基礎的遠端存取服務、主機式封包過濾、用於小型辦公室對等方的隧道實作、快速 spanning-tree、防火牆裝置平台及模擬器本身。每一項均自項目簡介及通用需求行得到正當依據，且每一道橋接均呈現為自所引一行所作的推論，而非引文。其中兩道橋接很強——標準指名 VRRP，而 Linux 的第一跳冗餘工具實作了它，且標準指名了該 spanning-tree 設計所滿足的交換層級——而一道較弱：用於站台對辦公室對等方的隧道，僅由該標準 VPN 那一行末尾的 `etc.` 所涵蓋，而報告如此說明，而非暗示有更強的涵蓋。
L1474 DIFF

The innovation work is presented as project innovation beyond the standard, anchored only on the standard's general professional requirements to maintain currency in a fast-changing industry and to research new methods — a legitimate anchor for *professional attributes*, and not a claim of coverage.
  ZH: 4. **創新線完全在該標準之外。** `Agentic AI`、`OpenClaw`、`MCP`、`artificial intelligence` 及 `machine learning` 在該標準中各出現**零次**，在擷取文字及原始標記中皆然。創新工作呈現為超越該標準的項目創新，僅錨定於該標準關於在快速變化的行業中保持與時並進及研究新方法的通用專業要求——這是*專業特質*的正當錨點，而非涵蓋範圍的主張。

L1490 DIFF
  EN: **Beyond the five demands.** Two further forms of value are worth stating. The first is **operational**: a five-person IT team cannot watch nineteen servers and sixteen network devices by hand, so the monitoring and logging stack, the automated health reporting, the scheduled configuration archive and the read-only triage assistance all reduce the manual attention the estate requires — and the proof-of-concept agent is deliberately read-only precisely so that it can be introduced into a production-adjacent environment without any risk of unsupervised change. The second is **reusability**: the runbooks, the version register with its confirmation commands, the configuration backup and restore procedure and the re-runnable automation mean the estate can be rebuilt or extended by someone who did not build it, which is the difference between a demonstration and an asset.
  ZH: **超越五項要求之外。** 另有兩種價值值得聲明。第一是**運作性**：一個五人的 IT 團隊無法以人手看管十九部伺服器及十六部網路裝置，因此監控與日誌堆疊、自動化健康狀態報告、排程配置歸檔及唯讀分類協助，全都減少該環境所需的人工關注——而概念驗證代理刻意設計為唯讀，正是為了使它可在無任何無人監督變更風險的情況下引入生產鄰近環境。第二是**可重用性**：runbook、附確認命令的版本登記冊、配置備份與還原程序，以及可重跑的自動化，意味著該環境可由並非建構它的人重建或擴充，這正是示範與資產之間的差別。
L1491 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L1492 DIFF
  EN: **What the value claim honestly excludes.** The design delivers **alignment with** selected controls, not certification, not a compliance attestation and not a penetration test. It is built and verified in a virtualised laboratory with no physical hardware, so it demonstrates design correctness, configuration correctness and documented behaviour rather than production performance. Those limits are stated in the report wherever a result is presented, and they are why the value argument is framed as a **defensible design and a reusable method** rather than as a deployed service.
  ZH: **價值主張誠實地排除什麼。** 本設計交付的是**與**所選控制項目**對齊**，而非認證、非合規鑑證，也非滲透測試。它是在沒有實體硬體的虛擬化實驗室中建成並驗證的，因此它展示的是設計正確性、配置正確性及已記錄行為，而非生產效能。這些限制在報告中呈現任何結果之處均予聲明，而它們正是價值論證被界定為**站得住腳的設計及可重用的方法**、而非已佈署服務的原因。

  EN: ## 18. Conclusion
  ZH: ## 18. 結論
L1514 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L1515 DIFF
  EN: This project will design, build, verify and document a virtualised enterprise network for a fictional Hong Kong financial-technology firm, in answer to a brief that asks students to *"study, build, configure, troubleshoot, and document complex enterprise networks"* across five named technology lines, and to *"produce well-organized documentation that includes all findings, manuals, steps, screen captures, verifications, and references related to the project."* Its aim is not merely to configure a set of services but to leave behind a **verified, reproducible and honestly documented** enterprise network: one where every capability claim traces to a test, every test to a command transcript or a screenshot, and every design decision to a stated requirement or an explicit, declared exclusion.
  ZH: 本項目將為一家虛構的香港金融科技公司設計、建構、驗證並記錄一套虛擬化企業網路，以回應一份要求學生*「study, build, configure, troubleshoot, and document complex enterprise networks」*、橫跨五條具名技術線，並*「produce well-organized documentation that includes all findings, manuals, steps, screen captures, verifications, and references related to the project.」*的簡介。其目的不僅是配置一組服務，而是留下一套**已驗證、可重現且誠實記錄**的企業網路：在其中，每一項能力主張都追溯至一項測試，每一項測試都追溯至一份命令逐字稿或一張截圖，而每一項設計決定都追溯至一項已聲明的要求，或一項明確且已聲明的排除。

  EN: 
  ZH: 
L1517 DIFF
  EN: **What will be built.** On a single student-owned hypervisor host, nineteen virtual machines — nine Windows Server guests and ten Linux guests, cloned from two prepared templates — will carry the whole service estate: one directory forest with two writable domain controllers and a read-only copy of identity at the branch; name resolution and addressing with redundancy and a documented failover relationship; file services with enforced quotas and file-type screening; network block storage with three logical units; a certificate authority on both platforms; certificate-based remote access implemented twice and independently; a two-node reverse proxy terminating transport security in front of a two-node application tier and a two-node content tier; host and network packet filtering with default-deny policy, address translation and logging; and a monitoring, logging and configuration-archive stack. Alongside it, sixteen simulated network devices across four sites will carry the Layer 2 and Layer 3 design: access, aggregation and core switching with explicit root selection and hardened access ports; interior and exterior gateway routing with a single tagged redistribution point; first-hop redundancy at both the switch layer and the Linux layer; and a firewall perimeter enforcing a sixteen-row trust-boundary table with address translation, inspection and centralised logging. On top of that estate, five PowerShell scripts, nine Ansible playbooks, seven roles and five Bash scripts will apply configuration as reviewed, version-controlled, idempotent code; and a read-only agent proof of concept will demonstrate, on real alerts, that triage can be shortened **without any write path to any device**.
  ZH: **將建成什麼。** 在單一學生自有的虛擬化平台主機上，十九部虛擬機——九個 Windows Server 客體及十個 Linux 客體，複製自兩個已備妥的範本——將承載整個服務環境：一個目錄樹系，含兩部可寫網域控制站及分支上的唯讀身分副本；具冗餘及已記錄故障轉移關係的名稱解析與定址；附強制配額及檔案類型屏蔽的檔案服務；含三個邏輯單元的網路區塊儲存；兩個平台上的憑證授權單位；以憑證為基礎的遠端存取實作兩次且各自獨立；一個雙節點反向代理在一個雙節點應用層及一個雙節點內容層之前終結傳輸安全；具預設拒絕政策、位址轉換及日誌的主機與網路封包過濾；以及一個監控、日誌及配置歸檔堆疊。與之並行，四個站台共十六部模擬網路裝置將承載第 2 層及第 3 層設計：附明確根橋選擇及強化存取埠的存取、彙總及核心交換；具單一帶標籤再分發點的內部及外部閘道路由；交換層及 Linux 層兩個層級的第一跳冗餘；以及一個強制執行十六列信任邊界表、具位址轉換、檢查及集中日誌的防火牆周邊。在該環境之上，五個 PowerShell 腳本、九個 Ansible playbook、七個角色及五個 Bash 腳本將以經審查、受版本控制、具冪等性的程式碼套用配置；而一個唯讀代理概念驗證將在真實告警上展示：分類可以**在對任何裝置均無任何寫入路徑的情況下**縮短。
L1518 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L1519 DIFF
  EN: **How it will be verified.** Verification is planned as first-class work rather than as a closing activity. One hundred and fifty-three test-case identifiers cover the four technology lines, the failover and resilience suite, the security suite, the performance method, the innovation line, the monitoring and certificate layers, and the documentation and governance workstreams. Six failover scenarios carry explicit numeric convergence thresholds that a virtualised laboratory can genuinely meet. The security suite tests every one of the sixteen trust-boundary rows in both directions and produces logged denial lines for the deny rows. The performance suite states plainly that it demonstrates a measurement method and detects regressions rather than measuring production capacity. And an objective-to-design-to-test-to-evidence traceability matrix with no empty cells is the artefact that makes the whole result auditable. Documentation and evidence are governed by a repository layout, a screenshot naming convention, a verification-record format, a findings register that is required to contain failures, and a master evidence index — because the project takes the view that **an unindexed, undated, unattributed screenshot is not evidence at all**, and that a capability which cannot be demonstrated has not been delivered.
  ZH: **將如何驗證。** 驗證被規劃為一級工作而非收尾活動。一百五十三個測試案例識別碼涵蓋四條技術線、故障轉移與韌性套件、安全套件、效能方法、創新線、監控與憑證層，以及文件與管治工作流。六個故障轉移情境帶有虛擬化實驗室真正能達成的明確數值收斂門檻。安全套件就十六列信任邊界逐列雙向測試，並為拒絕列產生已記錄的拒絕日誌行。效能套件明確聲明其展示的是一套量測方法並偵測迴歸，而非量測生產容量。而一個沒有空白儲存格的目標對設計對測試對證據追溯矩陣，正是使整個結果可稽核的產物。文件與證據由一套儲存庫佈局、一套截圖命名慣例、一套驗證記錄格式、一份被要求必須包含失敗的發現登記冊，以及一份主證據索引所管治——因為本項目採取這樣的立場：**一張未經索引、無日期、無歸屬的截圖根本不算證據**，而一項無法展示的能力並未交付。

  EN:

L1521 DIFF
  EN: **Standards alignment, stated honestly.** The design is aligned to the WorldSkills Occupational Standards 2026 for IT Network Systems Administration, and the alignment is specific: 75 % of the standard's weight sits in three 25 % sections — Data Transfer Networks, Network and System Operations and Troubleshooting — and this project is deliberately weighted the same way, with a correspondingly small share given to work organisation and communication, which the standard weights at 5 % each. The standard directly names the directory services, the web servers, the routing protocols, the redundancy protocols, the version-control and configuration-management tools, the scripting languages, the file-sharing protocols, the certificate services and the backup mechanisms this design uses, and the design quotes those lines where they apply. The gap disclosure matters just as much and is stated before it can be discovered: the standard contains **no time limit and no module structure**, so the twelve-week schedule is presented as the project's own client constraint and never as a standard requirement; the standard contains **no requirement for screen captures**, so the evidence set is presented as the brief's requirement and not the standard's; several technologies in this design — network block storage, file-server quota and screening, certificate-based remote-access service on the Microsoft platform, host-based packet filtering, the small-office tunnel implementation, rapid spanning-tree, the firewall platform and the simulator itself — are **not named by the standard**, and are justified from the brief and from generic requirement lines, with two strong protocol-level bridges and one deliberately labelled weak one; and the innovation line is **entirely outside** the standard, since the words describing it occur zero times, so it is presented as project innovation beyond the standard, anchored only on the standard's general professional requirement to maintain currency in a fast-changing industry. Presenting a bridge as a bridge, and an inference as an inference, is the specific discipline this proposal commits to, because an alignment claim that overreaches is worth less than no claim at all.
  ZH: **標準對齊，誠實聲明。** 本設計對齊 WorldSkills 職業標準 2026 IT Network Systems Administration，而該對齊是具體的：標準 75 % 的權重落在三個各佔 25 % 的章節——Data Transfer Networks、Network and System Operations 及 Troubleshooting——而本項目刻意以相同方式加權，並相應地給工作組織及溝通較小份額，該二者在標準中各佔 5 %。標準直接指名本設計所用的目錄服務、Web 伺服器、路由協定、冗餘協定、版本控制及配置管理工具、腳本語言、檔案共享協定、憑證服務及備份機制，而設計在適用處引用該等行。差距披露同樣重要，且在它被發現之前就已聲明：標準包含**沒有時限，也沒有模組結構**，因此十二週的進度表呈現為本項目自身的客戶限制，絕不呈現為標準要求；標準包含**沒有螢幕擷取要求**，因此證據集呈現為簡介的要求而非標準的要求；本設計中有若干技術——網路區塊儲存、檔案伺服器配額及屏蔽、Microsoft 平台上以憑證為基礎的遠端存取服務、主機式封包過濾、小型辦公室隧道實作、快速 spanning-tree、防火牆平台及模擬器本身——**未被標準指名**，且自簡介及通用需求行得到正當依據，其中兩道協定層級的橋接很強，一道刻意標示為弱；而創新線**完全在**標準之外，因為描述它的詞語出現零次，故它呈現為超越該標準的項目創新，僅錨定於該標準關於在快速變化的行業中保持與時並進的通用專業要求。把橋接呈現為橋接、把推論呈現為推論，正是本提案所承諾的特定紀律，因為一項過度延伸的對齊主張，比不作任何主張更沒價值。

  EN: 
  ZH: 
L1523 DIFF
  EN: **Why the project is worthwhile and achievable.** It is worthwhile because it is the shape of real work: an IT team of five people supporting 160 staff across four locations, under a contractual obligation to prove least privilege, auditability and failover, with no budget for new hardware — which is exactly the situation in which a well-designed virtualised estate, reviewed configuration-as-code and honest documentation are the difference between a system that can be defended in an audit and one that cannot. It is achievable within twelve weeks by four students with no physical hardware for four reasons, and each was designed in rather than assumed. **First, the scope was prioritised before the work began**, in a fixed four-band order with a pre-agreed cut sequence, so that if the schedule compresses it compresses predictably and the must band survives intact. **Second, the phases follow the service dependency chain**, so at most one phase is ever blocked and four people can genuinely work in parallel instead of queueing. **Third, the highest-uncertainty item is isolated and timeboxed**: the innovation line's runtime is exposed to a three-day feasibility spike whose output is a decision, and it is protected by designed fallbacks, so the least certain part of the project cannot take the rest of it down. **Fourth, and most importantly, the verification and documentation discipline is built into every task rather than added at the end** — evidence is part of the definition of done, the pairing arrangement means no member is a single point of failure, and the traceability matrix is maintained continuously, so that week 12 assembles a record that already exists rather than reconstructing one from memory. The project's honesty rules are part of the same design: version deviations are published, declared exclusions are listed and approved, the simulator's limits are labelled wherever a result touches them, and where the innovation line disappoints, the disappointment is documented as a finding. Delivering the must band with complete evidence, and stating plainly and precisely what was not delivered and why, is a complete and defensible outcome for a twelve-week final-year project — and it is the outcome this plan is built to produce.
  ZH: **為何本項目值得做且可達成。** 它值得做，因為它正是真實工作的形態：一個五人的 IT 團隊支持四個地點共 160 名員工，負有證明最小權限、可稽核性及故障轉移的合約義務，且沒有新硬體的預算——這正是精心設計的虛擬化環境、經審查的配置即程式碼及誠實的文件，是「能在稽核中站得住腳的系統」與「不能的系統」之間差別的那種處境。它可由四名學生在十二週內、在沒有實體硬體的情況下達成，理由有四，而每一項都是設計進去的而非假設的。**第一，範圍在工作開始之前已排定優先次序**，以固定的四帶順序及預先協定的削減序列，使進度表若被壓縮則可預期地壓縮，而「必須」帶完整存續。**第二，各階段依服務相依鏈排序**，因此任何時候最多只有一個階段被阻塞，四個人可以真正並行工作而非排隊。**第三，最高不確定性的項目被隔離並限時**：創新線的執行環境暴露於一項為期三日的可行性尖峰測試，其產出是一項決定，並由已設計的備援方案保護，使本項目最不確定的部分無法拖垮其餘部分。**第四，也是最重要的，驗證與文件紀律已內建於每項任務而非在最後附加**——證據是完成定義的一部分、配對安排意味著沒有成員是單點故障，而追溯矩陣持續維護，使第 12 週彙編的是一份已經存在的記錄，而非憑記憶重建一份。本項目的誠實規則屬於同一套設計：版本偏差予以公布、已聲明的排除項目予以列出並獲批准、模擬器的限制在其結果所涉及之處均予標註，而若創新線令人失望，該失望即記錄為一項發現。交付「必須」帶並附完整證據、且明確而精確地聲明什麼未交付及其原因，對一個十二週的畢業項目而言是完整且站得住腳的成果——而這正是本計劃所建構以產生的成果。

  EN: **URLs are given only where the URL is confirmed.** An address appears in this list only if it is present in one of the project's two canonical source documents: the design baseline (`proposal\00-design-baseline.md`) or the verified research pack (`proposal\research\wsos-2026-2447.md`). Both of those documents record how the source was retrieved and verified, so a URL carried from them is traceable. Every other entry is cited **by document title, issuing body and edition or version, without a URL**, because writing an address from memory is forbidden in this project and inventing one is worse than omitting it. Where an address could not be confirmed, the entry says so, or names where the source is archived, rather than pretending to a location.
  ZH: **僅在 URL 已獲確認時給出 URL。** 一個位址出現在本清單中，僅當它存在於本項目兩份權威來源文件之一：設計基準（`proposal\00-design-baseline.md`）或已核實的研究包（`proposal\research\wsos-2026-2447.md`）。該兩份文件均記錄該來源如何被取得及核實，故自其中帶出的 URL 可追溯。其他每一項條目均**以文件標題、發布機構及版次或版本引用，不附 URL**，因為在本項目中憑記憶寫出位址是被禁止的，而虛構一個比省略它更糟。若某個位址無法確認，該條目如此說明，或指明該來源的歸檔位置，而非假裝有一個位置。

L1533 DIFF
  EN: **Every entry was genuinely consulted, and consultation is recorded.** For each entry the reference register records who consulted it, when, and which sections cite it, and the consulted artefact is archived in the project's reference directory. A version number is cited only together with the command output that produced it.   EN: #### A.8 Name resolution records

L1737 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L1738 DIFF
  EN: Forward zone `vertexpeak.fyp.lab`, directory-integrated with secure dynamic updates only and seven-day scavenging. Every static host has an address record equal to its hostname. Additional records: `portal.vertexpeak.fyp.lab` → 10.10.90.10; `www.vertexpeak.fyp.lab` → 10.10.90.10; `blog.vertexpeak.fyp.lab` → 10.10.90.10; `vpn.vertexpeak.fyp.lab` → 10.10.50.10; `wg.vertexpeak.fyp.lab` → 10.10.50.10; `keepalived-hq` → 10.10.50.11 and 10.10.50.12; `keepalived-hq-web` → 10.10.90.10 (the nginx VIP, owned by no node, with `vpnz-dmzrev01` = 10.10.90.28 and `vpnz-dmzrev02` = 10.10.90.29); service-locator records for directory access, authentication and the global catalog created automatically by the directory service; and a conditional forwarder for the simulated public suffix pointing at 192.0.2.53. No autodiscover record is created, because no mail-system integration exists in this design and that exclusion is documented rather than left as an omission. The reverse zones `30.10.10.in-addr.arpa`, `40.10.10.in-addr.arpa`, `90.10.10.in-addr.arpa` and `20.20.10.in-addr.arpa` are directory-integrated with pointer records for all static hosts; the branch reverse zone is delegated to the read-only domain controller and forwarded from HQ.
  ZH: 正向區域 `vertexpeak.fyp.lab`，為目錄整合式，僅允許安全動態更新，清除期七日。每部靜態主機均有一筆與其主機名稱相同的位址記錄。其他記錄：`portal.vertexpeak.fyp.lab` → 10.10.90.10；`www.vertexpeak.fyp.lab` → 10.10.90.10；`blog.vertexpeak.fyp.lab` → 10.10.90.10；`vpn.vertexpeak.fyp.lab` → 10.10.50.10；`wg.vertexpeak.fyp.lab` → 10.10.50.10；`keepalived-hq` → 10.10.50.11 及 10.10.50.12；`keepalived-hq-web` → 10.10.90.10（nginx VIP，不屬於任何節點，其中 `vpnz-dmzrev01` = 10.10.90.28、`vpnz-dmzrev02` = 10.10.90.29）；用於目錄存取、驗證及通用類別目錄的服務定位記錄，由目錄服務自動建立；以及一個指向 192.0.2.53 的模擬公眾後綴條件式轉寄器。不建立任何 autodiscover 記錄，因為本設計中不存在任何郵件系統整合，而該排除予以記錄而非任其成為遺漏。反向區域 `30.10.10.in-addr.arpa`、`40.10.10.in-addr.arpa`、`90.10.10.in-addr.arpa` 及 `20.20.10.in-addr.arpa` 為目錄整合式，並載有所有靜態主機的指標記錄；分支反向區域已委派給唯讀網域控制站並自總部轉送。

  EN:

L1740 DIFF
  EN: #### A.9 Addressing redundancy rules
  ZH: #### A.9 定址冗餘規則
L1741 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L1742 DIFF
  EN: Every client receives 10.10.30.11 then 10.10.30.12 as its resolvers, in that order. The branch read-only domain controller is additionally a resolver for its site and sits third in the branch option list, and is never the only resolver for any client. The addressing relationship is primary to standby at 80/20 with a one-hour maximum client lead time and a 60-minute state switchover interval; branch delays must not exceed 500 ms or the branch fails over spuriously.
  ZH: 每個用戶端依序取得 10.10.30.11 然後 10.10.30.12 作為其解析器。分支唯讀網域控制站另為其站台的解析器，列於分支選項清單第三位，且絕不成為任何用戶端的唯一解析器。定址關係為主要對待命、80/20，最大用戶端前置時間一小時，狀態切換間隔 60 分鐘；分支延遲不得超過 500 毫秒，否則分支會誤觸故障轉移。

L1774 SAME

L1775 DIFF
  EN: 1. The native VLAN on every trunk is 999, never VLAN 1. VLAN 1 exists but carries no traffic.
  ZH: 1. 每個 trunk 的原生 VLAN 為 999，絕非 VLAN 1。VLAN 1 存在但不承載任何流量。
L1776 DIFF
  EN: 2. Every unused access port is assigned to VLAN 999 and shut down.
  ZH: 2. 每個未使用的存取埠均指派至 VLAN 999 並 shutdown。
L1777 DIFF
  EN: 3. `switchport nonegotiate` and `spanning-tree portfast` with `bpduguard enable` on all end-user access ports.
  ZH: 3. 所有終端使用者存取埠上啟用 `switchport nonegotiate` 及 `spanning-tree portfast` 加 `bpduguard enable`。
L1778 DIFF
  EN: 4. Trunks exist only between the two core switches, cores to access switches, cores to the edge router, cores to the firewall, and cores to the branch edge router.
  ZH: 4. Trunk 僅存在於兩部核心交換器之間、核心至存取交換器、核心至邊緣路由器、核心至防火牆，以及核心至分支邊緣路由器。
L1779 DIFF
  EN: 5. No VLAN is stretched between HQ and any branch; inter-site traffic is routed, not switched.
  ZH: 5. 沒有任何 VLAN 在總部與任何分支之間延伸；站間流量為路由而非交換。

L1800 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L1801 DIFF
  EN: The two mechanisms are **deliberately different and must not be merged into one claim**: the switch layer uses the vendor-native mechanism that the simulator supports reliably, and the Linux layer uses the open-source implementation of the standard protocol that the occupational standard names. Both pairs use health checks that test the *service* rather than the interface, so a running interface with a dead service still triggers a failover.
  ZH: 這兩種機制**刻意不同，且絕不得合併為一項主張**：交換器層使用模擬器能可靠支援的廠商原生機制，而 Linux 層使用職業標準所指名之標準協定的開源實作。兩組對均使用測試*服務*而非介面的健康檢查，因此介面仍在運行但服務已死時仍會觸發故障轉移。

  ZH:

L1846 DIFF
  EN: **Row count: 36 rows = 16 simulated network devices + 19 virtual machines + 1 hypervisor host.** Of the 19 virtual machines, **9 are Windows Server guests** and **10 are Linux guests**; the hypervisor host is physical and is not a guest. The four HQ access switches are four separate devices, not one. The optional, uncounted additions described in Appendices A and F — an issuing-authority guest and an optional tunnel-peer guest — are not included in the count of nineteen.
  ZH: **列數：36 列 = 16 部模擬網路裝置 + 19 部虛擬機 + 1 部虛擬化平台主機。** 19 部虛擬機中，**9 個為 Windows Server 客體**、**10 個為 Linux 客體**；虛擬化平台主機為實體且並非客體。四部總部存取交換器是四部獨立裝置，而非一部。附錄 A 及 F 所述的可選、未計入之增項——一個簽發授權單位客體及一個可選隧道對等方客體——不計入十九部之數。

L1858 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L1859 DIFF
  EN: **Allocation notes.** The 42-vCPU figure is *allocated* processor count, not simultaneous load; no guest is processor-saturated except briefly during package installation or an automation run, so an eight-core host is workable and a twelve-to-sixteen-core host is comfortable. Memory does not overcommit usefully on a workstation hypervisor, which makes the 78 GB of allocation the binding constraint and the reason the two-shift fallback exists. Every Windows guest except the domain controllers is a full clone of one prepared template and every Linux guest is a full clone of a second template; template disks are excluded from the running count while the templates are shut down, and that is what makes nineteen guests feasible on one host. All laboratory hardware addresses are hand-assigned within the hypervisor's own range and are ignored by the simulator.
  ZH: **分配註記。** 42 vCPU 的數字是*已分配*的處理器數量，而非同時負載；除了在套件安裝或自動化執行期間短暫出現之外，沒有客體處於處理器飽和狀態，因此八核心主機可行，十二至十六核心主機則綽綽有餘。記憶體在工作站虛擬化平台上無法有效超額承諾，這使 78 GB 的分配成為約束瓶頸，也是兩班制備援方案存在的原因。除網域控制站外，每個 Windows 客體均為一個已備妥範本的完整複本，而每個 Linux 客體均為第二個範本的完整複本；範本關機時其磁碟不計入運行數，而這正是使十九個客體可在單一主機上可行的原因。所有實驗室硬體位址均在虛擬化平台自身的範圍內手動指派，且被模擬器忽略。

L1903 DIFF
  EN: **Default-deny discipline.** Every row above is an entry in an allow-list. Any port not listed is denied in both directions, and the report contains one screenshot per zone boundary showing a denied packet, taken from the firewall log, the host filter's log prefix or the simulator's simulation mode.
  ZH: **預設拒絕的紀律。** 上述每一列均為一份允許清單中的條目。任何未列出的埠在兩個方向均被拒絕，而報告就每個區域邊界各含一張顯示被封包拒絕的截圖，取自防火牆日誌、主機過濾器的 log prefix 或模擬器的模擬模式。

  EN: ### Appendix E — Naming Conventions and Interface Contract
  ZH: ### Appendix E — 命名慣例及介面契約
L1906 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L1907 DIFF
  EN: The block below is the contract of values as issued. It is the anti-inconsistency mechanism of the project: values are copied verbatim and are never paraphrased, re-capitalised, re-addressed or "improved". A deviation from this block is a defect, and a change to it requires a decision-log entry, three-of-four group agreement, supervisor awareness and a re-issued revision of the design baseline.
  ZH: 以下區塊是所簽發的數值契約。它是本項目的反不一致機制：數值逐字複製，且永不轉述、永不被重新調整大小寫、永不被重新定址，也永不被「改良」。偏離此區塊即屬缺陷，而對它的變更需要決策日誌記錄、四人中三人的小組同意、導師知情，以及重新簽發設計基準的修訂版本。

L2133 DIFF
  EN: Hostnames are at most fifteen characters, contain no underscores, and are never re-capitalised: the strings above are canonical. The Linux estate uses lower-case RFC 1123 syntax, and administrators always log in with their own named account, never as the superuser and never with a shared account.
  ZH: 主機名稱最多十五個字元、不含底線，且永不重新調整大小寫：以上字串即為權威。Linux 環境使用小寫 RFC 1123 語法，而管理員一律以自己具名的帳戶登入，絕不以超級使用者身分，也絕不使用共用帳戶。

  EN: ### Appendix F — Software and Version Register
  ZH: ### Appendix F — 軟件及版本登記冊
L2136 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L2137 DIFF
  EN: Every version is re-verified at build time with a version-printing command, and the actual output is captured as evidence. Where the actual version differs from this register, the report contains a version-deviations subsection listing each difference and its impact; documenting a version that does not match the running system is treated as an integrity failure for this project.
  ZH: 每個版本均在建構時以列印版本的命令重新核實，並將實際輸出擷取為證據。若實際版本與本登記冊不同，報告即載有一個版本偏差小節，列出每項差異及其影響；對本項目而言，記錄一個與運行系統不符的版本被視為完整性失敗。

L2169 DIFF
  EN: **Version-deviation record.** Differences between this register and the running systems are listed in the report's version-deviations subsection, each with the impact statement required by the pinning discipline. The record is a table with columns for component, pinned version, observed version, the command that produced the observation, the impact, and whether the deviation changes any design assumption.
  ZH: **版本偏差記錄。** 本登記冊與運行系統之間的差異列於報告的版本偏差小節，每項均附鎖定紀律所要求的影響陳述。該記錄是一份表格，欄位包括元件、已鎖定版本、觀察到的版本、產生該觀察的命令、影響，以及該偏差是否改變任何設計假設。

  EN: ### Appendix I — Configuration Backup and Restore Procedure
  ZH: ### Appendix I — 配置備份與還原程序
L2363 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L2364 DIFF
  EN: **Scope statement.** This is a **configuration and evidence** backup. It is explicitly **not** a full virtual-machine or hypervisor-level backup, because the project has no hypervisor-level backup licence and no shared storage fabric. The report states this boundary wherever the backup design is described, so that no reader infers a restore capability that does not exist. Whole-machine recovery is by re-cloning from the templates and re-applying the automation, which is why the runbooks and the idempotent playbooks are themselves the primary recovery asset.
  ZH: **範圍聲明。** 這是**配置及證據**備份。它明確**不是**完整的虛擬機或虛擬化平台層級備份，因為本項目沒有虛擬化平台層級備份授權，也沒有共享儲存架構。報告在描述備份設計的每一處均聲明此界線，使任何讀者都不會推斷出一個並不存在的還原能力。整機復原的方式是自範本重新複製並重新套用自動化，這就是為何 runbook 及具冪等性的 playbook 本身就是主要的復原資產。

L2378 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L2379 DIFF
  EN: **Restore verification, and why it is mandatory.** A backup that has never been restored is an assumption. The project therefore times and documents four restores and treats them as test evidence rather than as housekeeping: a single file restored from the off-host repository; a deleted organisational unit restored from the directory recycle bin; a file restored to an alternate location from the file-service backup; and a scratch virtual machine rebuilt from a template plus automation. Each restore records the elapsed time, because elapsed time is the only meaningful measure of whether a backup procedure works. The storage-target restart test (`TC-FO-08`) and the optional disaster-recovery exercise both depend on these procedures, so the procedures are exercised before they are needed rather than during an incident.
  ZH: **還原驗證，以及為何它是強制性的。** 從未還原過的備份是一項假設。因此本項目計時並記錄四次還原，並將其視為測試證據而非日常雜務：自離主機儲存庫還原的單一檔案；自目錄資源回收筒還原的已刪除組織單位；自檔案服務備份還原至替代位置的檔案；以及自範本加上自動化重建的臨時虛擬機。每次還原均記錄經過時間，因為經過時間是衡量備份程序是否有效的唯一有意義指標。儲存目標重啟測試（`TC-FO-08`）及可選的災難復原演練均依賴這些程序，因此這些程序在需要之前即已演練，而非在事故期間才演練。
L2380 SAME
  EN: 
  ZH: 
L2381 DIFF

L2388 DIFF
  EN: | Layer 1 — Physical | Transmission of raw bits over a medium: cabling, connectors, signalling, power | Layer 1 |

L2393 DIFF
  EN: | Layer 6 — Presentation | Data representation: encoding, compression and encryption formats | Layer 6 |

L2397 DIFF

  ZH: | Packet | 第 3 層協定資料單元，帶來源及目的邏輯位址 | 第 3 層 |
L2398 DIFF
  EN: | Segment | A Layer 4 protocol data unit in a connection-oriented transport protocol | Layer 4 |
  ZH: | Segment | 連線導向傳輸協定中的第 4 層協定資料單元 | 第 4 層 |

L2622 DIFF
  EN: The two rows illustrate the two properties the log exists to enforce: that a change in score is backed by an observation rather than by opinion, and that closing a risk does not mean pretending the underlying uncertainty has gone away — it moves to the risk that actually carries it.
